-- Database: aerospace_engine_performance_monitoring_and_analysis

CREATE TABLE test_campaigns (
  campaign_id INTEGER ,
  campaign_name TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  objective TEXT ,
  engine_config TEXT ,
  test_site TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (campaign_id)
);

CREATE TABLE engine_models (
  model_id INTEGER ,
  model_name TEXT ,
  manufacturer TEXT ,
  specifications TEXT ,
  engine_type TEXT ,
  thrust_class TEXT ,
  operating_conditions TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_sessions (
  session_id INTEGER ,
  campaign_id INTEGER ,
  model_id INTEGER ,
  session_start TEXT ,
  session_end TEXT ,
  test_conditions TEXT ,
  session_status TEXT ,
  remarks TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (campaign_id) REFERENCES test_campaigns (campaign_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (model_id) REFERENCES engine_models (model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_conditions (
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  altitude REAL ,
  ambient_temperature REAL ,
  ambient_pressure REAL ,
  humidity REAL ,
  weather_conditions TEXT ,
  atmospheric_conditions TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (condition_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  access_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  reading_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  access_method TEXT ,
  access_location TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_equipment (
  equipment_id INTEGER ,
  equipment_name TEXT ,
  type TEXT ,
  manufacturer TEXT ,
  model TEXT ,
  equipment_status TEXT ,
  calibration_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (equipment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_session_equipment (
  session_equipment_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  equipment_id INTEGER ,
  equipment_role TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_equipment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (equipment_id) REFERENCES test_equipment (equipment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE data_files (
  file_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  file_path TEXT ,
  file_size REAL ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  file_format TEXT ,
  file_checksum TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (file_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE readme_files (
  readme_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  file_path TEXT ,
  file_size REAL ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  file_format TEXT ,
  file_checksum TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (readme_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_notes (
  note_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  note_text TEXT ,
  note_date TEXT ,
  note_type TEXT ,
  note_status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (note_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_anomalies (
  anomaly_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  anomaly_type TEXT ,
  anomaly_description TEXT ,
  anomaly_date TEXT ,
  anomaly_severity TEXT ,
  anomaly_resolution TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (anomaly_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_reports (
  report_id INTEGER ,
  session_id INTEGER ,
  report_date TEXT ,
  report_file TEXT ,
  report_summary TEXT ,
  report_type TEXT ,
  report_status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (report_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES test_sessions (session_id)
);

-- Database: aerospace_engineering_and_aerodynamics_data_collection

CREATE TABLE experiments (
  experiment_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  type TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  lead_researcher_id INTEGER ,
  location TEXT ,
  budget REAL ,
  funding_source TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (experiment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE datasets (
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  experiment_id INTEGER ,
  pwm REAL ,
  power REAL ,
  net_force REAL ,
  voltage REAL ,
  current REAL ,
  version_id INTEGER ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  last_modified_date TEXT ,
  file_format TEXT ,
  file_size REAL ,
  checksum TEXT ,
  tags TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (experiment_id) REFERENCES experiments (experiment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (version_id) REFERENCES versions (version_id)
);

CREATE TABLE experiment_conditions (
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  experiment_id INTEGER ,
  parameter TEXT ,
  value REAL ,
  unit TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (condition_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (experiment_id) REFERENCES experiments (experiment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE parameters (
  parameter_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  unit TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  range_min REAL ,
  range_max REAL ,
  default_value REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (parameter_id)
);

CREATE TABLE readme_files (
  readme_id INTEGER ,
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  version_id INTEGER ,
  file_path TEXT ,
  file_size REAL ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  last_modified_date TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (readme_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (version_id) REFERENCES versions (version_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  last_login_date TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE versions (
  version_id INTEGER ,
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  version_number INTEGER ,
  version_date TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  last_modified_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (version_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: agricultural_crop_simulation_and_treatment_analysis

CREATE TABLE simulations (
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_name TEXT ,
  crop_type_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  location_id INTEGER ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  soil_type_id INTEGER ,
  weather_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (crop_type_id) REFERENCES crop_types (crop_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (location_id) REFERENCES locations (location_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (soil_type_id) REFERENCES soil_types (soil_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (weather_id) REFERENCES weather_conditions (weather_id)
);

CREATE TABLE treatments (
  treatment_id INTEGER ,
  treatment_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  application_rate REAL ,
  application_method_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (treatment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (application_method_id) REFERENCES treatment_methods (method_id)
);

CREATE TABLE growth_stages (
  growth_stage_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  firstsquare_DAS_50pc REAL ,
  firstflower_DAS_50pc REAL ,
  harvest_DAS_50pc REAL ,
  height REAL ,
  leaf_area_index REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (growth_stage_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (simulation_id) REFERENCES simulations (simulation_id)
);

CREATE TABLE simulation_treatments (
  simulation_treatment_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  treatment_id INTEGER ,
  application_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (simulation_treatment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (simulation_id) REFERENCES simulations (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (treatment_id) REFERENCES treatments (treatment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE simulation_access (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  duration INTEGER ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (simulation_id) REFERENCES simulations (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE crop_types (
  crop_type_id INTEGER ,
  crop_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  optimal_soil_ph REAL ,
  optimal_temperature_range TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (crop_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE locations (
  location_id INTEGER ,
  location_name TEXT ,
  region TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  elevation INTEGER ,
  climate_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (location_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weather_conditions (
  weather_id INTEGER ,
  weather_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  average_temperature REAL ,
  precipitation REAL ,
  humidity REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (weather_id)
);

CREATE TABLE soil_types (
  soil_type_id INTEGER ,
  soil_type_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  ph_range TEXT ,
  texture TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (soil_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE treatment_methods (
  method_id INTEGER ,
  method_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (method_id)
);

-- Database: agricultural_or_ecological_simulation_parameters

CREATE TABLE simulations (
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  season_id INTEGER ,
  growth_rate TEXT ,
  ecological_interaction TEXT ,
  seeding_strategy INTEGER ,
  start_month INTEGER ,
  moore_neighborhood INTEGER ,
  suitability_threshold TEXT ,
  latency_period INTEGER ,
  std_dev_env_factor_a TEXT ,
  local_effect_env_factor_a TEXT ,
  long_range_effect_env_factor_a TEXT ,
  cluster_formation INTEGER ,
  climate_condition_id INTEGER ,
  soil_type_id INTEGER ,
  crop_id INTEGER ,
  water_source_id INTEGER ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  updated_by INTEGER ,
  updated_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (season_id) REFERENCES seasons (season_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (climate_condition_id) REFERENCES climate_conditions (climate_condition_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (soil_type_id) REFERENCES soil_types (soil_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (crop_id) REFERENCES crops (crop_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (water_source_id) REFERENCES water_sources (water_source_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE seasons (
  season_id INTEGER ,
  season_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  start_month INTEGER ,
  end_month INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (season_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  updated_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE simulation_results (
  result_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  run_date TEXT ,
  outcome TEXT ,
  yield TEXT ,
  ecosystem_stability TEXT ,
  water_usage TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  updated_by INTEGER ,
  updated_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (result_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (simulation_id) REFERENCES simulations (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE climate_conditions (
  climate_condition_id INTEGER ,
  climate_condition_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  temperature TEXT ,
  precipitation TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (climate_condition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE soil_types (
  soil_type_id INTEGER ,
  soil_type_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  ph_level TEXT ,
  nutrient_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (soil_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE crops (
  crop_id INTEGER ,
  crop_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  growth_rate TEXT ,
  water_requirement TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (crop_id)
);

CREATE TABLE water_sources (
  water_source_id INTEGER ,
  water_source_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  capacity TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (water_source_id)
);

CREATE TABLE collaborations (
  collaboration_id INTEGER ,
  simulation_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  updated_by INTEGER ,
  updated_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (collaboration_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (simulation_id) REFERENCES simulations (simulation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: air_filter_specifications_and_performance_tracking

CREATE TABLE filter_models (
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  model_name TEXT ,
  filter_class TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  manufacturer_id INTEGER ,
  production_date TEXT ,
  warranty_period INTEGER ,
  material TEXT ,
  color TEXT ,
  weight REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE filter_classes (
  filter_class_id INTEGER ,
  filter_class TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  min_efficiency REAL ,
  max_efficiency REAL ,
  recommended_use TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (filter_class_id)
);

CREATE TABLE filter_dimensions (
  dimension_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  width INTEGER ,
  height INTEGER ,
  depth INTEGER ,
  volume REAL ,
  surface_area REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dimension_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE filter_performance (
  performance_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  nominal_airflow REAL ,
  initial_pressure_drop REAL ,
  final_pressure_drop REAL ,
  maximum_airflow REAL ,
  efficiency REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (performance_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pocket_numbers (
  pocket_number_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  pocket_number INTEGER ,
  pocket_shape TEXT ,
  pocket_material TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pocket_number_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE filtration_areas (
  filtration_area_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  filtration_area REAL ,
  effective_filtration_area REAL ,
  filtration_media TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (filtration_area_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE testing_data (
  testing_data_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  test_date TEXT ,
  pressure_drop REAL ,
  airflow REAL ,
  test_location TEXT ,
  test_operator_id INTEGER ,
  test_notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (testing_data_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE certifications (
  certification_id INTEGER ,
  filter_model_id INTEGER ,
  certification_name TEXT ,
  regulation TEXT ,
  certification_date TEXT ,
  expiration_date TEXT ,
  certifying_body TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (certification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (filter_model_id) REFERENCES filter_models (filter_model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: apparel_size_measurement_and_inventory_tracking

CREATE TABLE sizes (
  size_id INTEGER ,
  size_label TEXT ,
  numeric_size TEXT ,
  bust TEXT ,
  waist TEXT ,
  hips TEXT ,
  category_id INTEGER ,
  shoulder TEXT ,
  length TEXT ,
  sleeve_length TEXT ,
  inseam TEXT ,
  material TEXT ,
  fit_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE products (
  product_id INTEGER ,
  product_name TEXT ,
  category_id INTEGER ,
  brand_id INTEGER ,
  description TEXT ,
  price REAL ,
  color TEXT ,
  gender TEXT ,
  material TEXT ,
  fit_type TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  last_updated_at TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES categories (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE categories (
  category_id INTEGER ,
  category_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  parent_category_id INTEGER ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE inventory (
  inventory_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  quantity_available INTEGER ,
  location_id INTEGER ,
  last_stock_check_date TEXT ,
  is_low_stock_alert INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (inventory_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE returns (
  return_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  return_reason TEXT ,
  return_status TEXT ,
  return_amount REAL ,
  return_shipment_date TEXT ,
  return_tracking_number TEXT ,
  return_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (return_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES customers (customer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE customers (
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone TEXT ,
  address_id INTEGER ,
  date_of_birth TEXT ,
  gender TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (customer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE orders (
  order_id INTEGER ,
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  order_date TEXT ,
  total_amount REAL ,
  shipping_method TEXT ,
  order_status TEXT ,
  is_gift INTEGER ,
  gift_message TEXT ,
  last_updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES customers (customer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE order_items (
  order_item_id INTEGER ,
  order_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  quantity INTEGER ,
  unit_price REAL ,
  discount_amount REAL ,
  final_price REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders (order_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE shipping (
  shipping_id INTEGER ,
  order_id INTEGER ,
  shipping_address TEXT ,
  shipping_method TEXT ,
  shipping_cost REAL ,
  estimated_delivery_date TEXT ,
  actual_delivery_date TEXT ,
  is_delivered INTEGER ,
  shipping_date TEXT ,
  tracking_number TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (shipping_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders (order_id)
);

CREATE TABLE payments (
  payment_id INTEGER ,
  order_id INTEGER ,
  payment_date TEXT ,
  amount REAL ,
  payment_method TEXT ,
  payment_status TEXT ,
  is_refunded INTEGER ,
  refund_amount REAL ,
  last_updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (payment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders (order_id)
);

CREATE TABLE promotions (
  promotion_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  discount_percentage REAL ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  promotion_type TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  last_updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (promotion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE product_images (
  image_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  is_primary INTEGER ,
  image_alt_text TEXT ,
  image_type TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (image_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  action TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  log_type TEXT ,
  log_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  full_name TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  last_login_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

-- Database: apparel_size_measurement_data

CREATE TABLE sizes (
  size_id INTEGER ,
  size_label TEXT ,
  size_category TEXT ,
  size_group TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE measurements (
  measurement_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  bust TEXT ,
  shoulder TEXT ,
  sleeve TEXT ,
  length TEXT ,
  measurement_unit TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (measurement_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE clothing_items (
  item_id INTEGER ,
  item_name TEXT ,
  item_type TEXT ,
  item_sub_type TEXT ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  material TEXT ,
  color TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (item_id)
);

CREATE TABLE clothing_item_sizes (
  item_size_id INTEGER ,
  item_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (item_size_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_id) REFERENCES clothing_items (item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE item_categories (
  category_id INTEGER ,
  category_name TEXT ,
  category_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE item_sub_categories (
  sub_category_id INTEGER ,
  sub_category_name TEXT ,
  sub_category_description TEXT ,
  category_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sub_category_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES item_categories (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE customer_preferences (
  preference_id INTEGER ,
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  item_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  preference_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (preference_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_id) REFERENCES clothing_items (item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

  PRIMARY KEY (size_id)
);

  product_id INTEGER ,

  brand TEXT ,
  price REAL ,
  inventory_count INTEGER ,
  product_description TEXT ,
  material TEXT ,
  color TEXT ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE categories (
  category_id INTEGER ,

  description TEXT ,
  parent_category_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (category_id)
);

  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE size_analysis (
  analysis_id INTEGER ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  sales_count INTEGER ,
  report_date TEXT ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (analysis_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id)
);

CREATE TABLE inventory_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  change_amount INTEGER ,
  change_date TEXT ,
  reason TEXT ,
  size_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (size_id) REFERENCES sizes (size_id)
);

CREATE TABLE products_reviews (
  review_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  rating INTEGER ,
  review_date TEXT ,
  review_text TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (review_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE product_recommendations (
  recommendation_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  product_id INTEGER ,
  reason TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (recommendation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES products (product_id)
);

-- Database: benchmarking_and_performance_analysis_of_multithreaded_applications

  name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (scenario_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_configurations (
  config_id INTEGER ,
  scenario_id INTEGER ,
  array_size_kb INTEGER ,
  thread_count INTEGER ,
  access_pattern_id INTEGER ,
  run_iterations INTEGER ,
  warmup_iterations INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (config_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (scenario_id) REFERENCES benchmark_scenarios (scenario_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (access_pattern_id) REFERENCES access_patterns (pattern_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_patterns (
  pattern_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pattern_id)
);

CREATE TABLE performance_metrics (
  metric_id INTEGER ,
  config_id INTEGER ,
  cpu_time_ns REAL ,
  other_metrics TEXT ,
  memory_usage_mb INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (metric_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (config_id) REFERENCES test_configurations (config_id)
);

CREATE TABLE results (
  result_id INTEGER ,
  config_id INTEGER ,
  metric_id INTEGER ,
  result_value REAL ,
  unit TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (result_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (config_id) REFERENCES test_configurations (config_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (metric_id) REFERENCES performance_metrics (metric_id)
);

CREATE TABLE threads (
  thread_id INTEGER ,
  config_id INTEGER ,
  thread_name TEXT ,
  thread_priority INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (thread_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (config_id) REFERENCES test_configurations (config_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_runs (
  run_id INTEGER ,
  config_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  start_time TEXT ,
  end_time TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (run_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (config_id) REFERENCES test_configurations (config_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE benchmark_tags (
  tag_id INTEGER ,
  scenario_id INTEGER ,
  tag_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tag_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (scenario_id) REFERENCES benchmark_scenarios (scenario_id)
);

CREATE TABLE test_run_metrics (
  run_metric_id INTEGER ,
  run_id INTEGER ,
  metric_id INTEGER ,
  result_value REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (run_metric_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES test_runs (run_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (metric_id) REFERENCES performance_metrics (metric_id)
);

-- Database: bicycle_trip_data_analysis_and_user_behavior_tracking

  end_station_id INTEGER ,

  FOREIGN KEY (end_station_id) REFERENCES stations (station_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (bike_id) REFERENCES bikes (bike_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)

-- Database: city_and_population_data_management

CREATE TABLE cities (
  city_id INTEGER ,
  city_name TEXT ,
  city_ascii TEXT ,
  state_id TEXT ,
  county_fips INTEGER ,
  lat REAL ,
  lng REAL ,
  population INTEGER ,
  density INTEGER ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  ranking INTEGER ,
  city_description TEXT ,
  elevation REAL ,
  area REAL ,
  founding_date TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (city_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (state_id) REFERENCES states (state_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (county_fips) REFERENCES counties (county_fips), 
  FOREIGN KEY (timezone) REFERENCES timezones (timezone_id)
);

CREATE TABLE states (
  state_id TEXT ,
  state_name TEXT ,
  state_abbr TEXT ,
  capital TEXT ,
  population INTEGER ,
  area REAL ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  state_description TEXT ,
  capital_lat REAL ,
  capital_lng REAL ,
  state_flag_url TEXT ,
  state_motto TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (state_id)
);

CREATE TABLE counties (
  county_fips INTEGER ,
  county_name TEXT ,
  state_id TEXT ,
  area REAL ,
  population INTEGER ,
  density INTEGER ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  county_description TEXT ,
  county_seat TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,

);

CREATE TABLE timezones (
  timezone_id TEXT ,
  timezone_name TEXT ,
  gmt_offset INTEGER ,
  dst_offset INTEGER ,
  description TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (timezone_id)
);

CREATE TABLE population_history (
  history_id INTEGER ,
  city_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  population INTEGER ,
  source TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (history_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (city_id) REFERENCES cities (city_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (source) REFERENCES data_sources (source_name)
);

CREATE TABLE density_history (
  history_id INTEGER ,
  city_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  density INTEGER ,
  source TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (history_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (city_id) REFERENCES cities (city_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (source) REFERENCES data_sources (source_name)
);

CREATE TABLE city_rankings (
  ranking_id INTEGER ,
  city_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  ranking INTEGER ,
  criteria TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (ranking_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (city_id) REFERENCES cities (city_id)

-- Database: cognitive_task_performance_and_analysis

CREATE TABLE participants (
  participant_id TEXT ,

  trial_id INTEGER ,

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  competitor_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (competitor_id) REFERENCES competitors (competitor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE market_trends (
  trend_id INTEGER ,
  trend_title TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  impact_score INTEGER ,
  trend_category TEXT ,
  related_competitors TEXT ,
  impact_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (trend_id)
);

-- Database: contact_lens_product_catalog_and_inventory_management

CREATE TABLE contact_lenses (
  lens_id INTEGER ,
  lens_name TEXT ,
  power_range TEXT ,
  base_curve REAL ,
  diameter REAL ,
  material TEXT ,
  water_content TEXT ,
  wearing_type TEXT ,
  price REAL ,
  color TEXT ,
  brand TEXT ,
  package_size INTEGER ,
  uv_protection INTEGER ,
  is_toric INTEGER ,
  is_multifocal INTEGER ,
  supplier_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (lens_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (supplier_id) REFERENCES suppliers (supplier_id)
);

CREATE TABLE inventory (
  inventory_id INTEGER ,
  lens_id INTEGER ,
  stock_quantity INTEGER ,
  reorder_level INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  reorder_quantity INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (inventory_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (lens_id) REFERENCES contact_lenses (lens_id)
);

CREATE TABLE suppliers (
  supplier_id INTEGER ,
  supplier_name TEXT ,
  contact_info TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  phone TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  contact_person TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (supplier_id)
);

CREATE TABLE order_items (
  order_item_id INTEGER ,
  order_id INTEGER ,
  lens_id INTEGER ,
  quantity INTEGER ,
  price REAL ,
  promotion_id INTEGER ,
  discount_amount REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (lens_id) REFERENCES contact_lenses (lens_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (promotion_id) REFERENCES promotions (promotion_id)
);

CREATE TABLE customers (
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  date_of_birth TEXT ,
  account_creation_date TEXT ,
  last_purchase_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (customer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sales (
  sale_id INTEGER ,
  order_id INTEGER ,
  sale_date TEXT ,
  total_amount REAL ,
  order_source TEXT ,
  sales_representative_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sale_id)
);

CREATE TABLE returns (
  return_id INTEGER ,
  order_item_id INTEGER ,
  return_date TEXT ,
  quantity INTEGER ,
  reason TEXT ,
  refund_amount REAL ,
  return_method TEXT ,
  return_status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (return_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES order_items (order_item_id)
);

CREATE TABLE promotions (
  promotion_id INTEGER ,
  lens_id INTEGER ,
  promotion_description TEXT ,
  discount_percentage REAL ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  promotion_type TEXT ,
  max_discount REAL ,
  minimum_spend REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (promotion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (lens_id) REFERENCES contact_lenses (lens_id)
);

CREATE TABLE feedback (
  feedback_id INTEGER ,
  customer_id INTEGER ,
  lens_id INTEGER ,
  rating INTEGER ,
  comments TEXT ,
  feedback_date TEXT ,
  is_public INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (feedback_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (customer_id) REFERENCES customers (customer_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (lens_id) REFERENCES contact_lenses (lens_id)
);

-- Database: criminal_justice_and_law_enforcement_682248

CREATE TABLE arrest_records (
  arrest_id INTEGER ,
  arrest_date TEXT ,
  booking_date TEXT ,
  arrestee_id INTEGER ,
  arrest_location_id INTEGER ,
  charge_id INTEGER ,
  arresting_officer_id INTEGER ,
  incident_number TEXT ,
  arrest_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (arrest_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (arrestee_id) REFERENCES arrestees (arrestee_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (arrest_location_id) REFERENCES arrest_locations (location_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (charge_id) REFERENCES charges (charge_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (arresting_officer_id) REFERENCES officers (officer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE arrestees (
  arrestee_id INTEGER ,
  sex TEXT ,
  race TEXT ,
  address_city TEXT ,
  address_state TEXT ,
  address_zip INTEGER ,
  date_of_birth TEXT ,
  social_security_number TEXT ,
  driver_license_number TEXT ,
  contact_phone TEXT ,
  contact_email TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (arrestee_id)
);

CREATE TABLE arrest_locations (
  location_id INTEGER ,
  location_name TEXT ,
  location_address TEXT ,
  location_city TEXT ,
  location_state TEXT ,
  location_zip INTEGER ,
  location_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (location_id)
);

CREATE TABLE charges (
  charge_id INTEGER ,
  charge_code INTEGER ,
  charge_wording TEXT ,
  charge_level TEXT ,
  statute_code TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (charge_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dispositions (
  disposition_id INTEGER ,
  charge_id INTEGER ,
  disposition_date TEXT ,
  disposition_type TEXT ,
  disposition_details TEXT ,
  court_case_number TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (disposition_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (charge_id) REFERENCES charges (charge_id)
);

CREATE TABLE case_outcomes (
  outcome_id INTEGER ,
  disposition_id INTEGER ,
  outcome_date TEXT ,
  outcome_type TEXT ,
  sentence TEXT ,
  probation_terms TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (outcome_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disposition_id) REFERENCES dispositions (disposition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE law_enforcement_units (
  unit_id INTEGER ,
  unit_name TEXT ,
  unit_description TEXT ,
  unit_contact TEXT ,
  unit_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (unit_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  unit_id INTEGER ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  last_login_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES law_enforcement_units (unit_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  record_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  action_performed TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (record_id) REFERENCES arrest_records (arrest_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE officers (
  officer_id INTEGER ,
  officer_name TEXT ,
  badge_number INTEGER ,
  rank TEXT ,
  unit_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (officer_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES law_enforcement_units (unit_id)
);

CREATE TABLE arrest_officers (
  arrest_id INTEGER ,
  officer_id INTEGER ,
  role TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (arrest_id, officer_id)
);

  PRIMARY KEY (trade_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (crypto_id) REFERENCES cryptocurrencies (id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES order_history (order_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,

  email TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,

  last_login TEXT ,

  updated_at TEXT ,
  endpoint_url TEXT ,

  api_key TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)

-- Database: document_comparison_and_analysis_for_software_development

CREATE TABLE documents (
  document_id INTEGER ,
  document_name TEXT ,
  version_number INTEGER ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  author_id INTEGER ,
  document_type TEXT ,
  file_path TEXT ,
  file_size INTEGER ,
  last_modified TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (document_id),

CREATE TABLE authors (
  author_id INTEGER ,
  author_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  position TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (author_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comparisons (
  comparison_id INTEGER ,
  document_id_1 INTEGER ,
  document_id_2 INTEGER ,
  comparison_method TEXT ,
  similarity_score REAL ,
  average_time REAL ,
  status TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (comparison_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (document_id_1) REFERENCES documents (document_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (document_id_2) REFERENCES documents (document_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comparison_method) REFERENCES comparison_methods (method_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comparison_methods (
  method_id INTEGER ,
  method_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  algorithm TEXT ,
  parameters TEXT ,
  version TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (method_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comparison_results (
  result_id INTEGER ,
  comparison_id INTEGER ,
  metric_name TEXT ,
  metric_value REAL ,
  metric_id INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (result_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comparison_id) REFERENCES comparisons (comparison_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (metric_id) REFERENCES metrics (metric_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (
  role_id INTEGER ,
  role_name TEXT ,
  permissions TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,

  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES user_roles (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comparison_history (
  history_id INTEGER ,
  comparison_id INTEGER ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  comparison_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (history_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comparison_id) REFERENCES comparisons (comparison_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE metrics (
  metric_id INTEGER ,
  metric_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  unit TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (metric_id)
);

CREATE TABLE error_logs (
  error_id INTEGER ,
  comparison_id INTEGER ,
  error_message TEXT ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  error_code TEXT ,
  severity TEXT ,
  resolved_by INTEGER ,
  resolved_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (error_id),

  description TEXT ,

-- Database: experimental_behavioral_data_collection_and_analysis

CREATE TABLE subjects (
  subject_id INTEGER ,
  subject_code TEXT ,
  age INTEGER ,
  gender TEXT ,
  enrollment_date TEXT ,
  last_participation_date TEXT ,
  ethnicity TEXT ,
  education_level TEXT ,
  occupation TEXT ,
  medical_history TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (subject_id)
);

CREATE TABLE trials (
  trial_id INTEGER ,
  subject_id INTEGER ,
  trial_number INTEGER ,
  reaction_time INTEGER ,
  choice INTEGER ,
  item_value_0 REAL ,
  item_value_1 REAL ,
  gaze_0 REAL ,
  gaze_1 REAL ,
  session_type TEXT ,
  gaze_L_bin3 REAL ,
  gaze_R_bin3 REAL ,
  trial_og INTEGER ,
  trial_description TEXT ,
  trial_conditions TEXT ,
  stimulus_type TEXT ,
  stimulus_duration INTEGER ,
  response_device TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (trial_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects (subject_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_value_0) REFERENCES items (value), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_value_1) REFERENCES items (value), 
  FOREIGN KEY (trial_og) REFERENCES trials (trial_number)
);

CREATE TABLE sessions (
  session_id INTEGER ,
  subject_id INTEGER ,
  session_type TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  session_code TEXT ,
  session_description TEXT ,
  session_notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects (subject_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_type) REFERENCES session_types (session_type_name)
);

CREATE TABLE items (
  item_id INTEGER ,

  image_path TEXT ,

  item_subcategory TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (item_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (trial_id) REFERENCES trials (trial_id)
);

CREATE TABLE choices (
  choice_id INTEGER ,
  trial_id INTEGER ,
  choice INTEGER ,
  item_id_0 INTEGER ,
  item_id_1 INTEGER ,
  choice_confidence REAL ,
  choice_reaction_time INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (choice_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (trial_id) REFERENCES trials (trial_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_id_0) REFERENCES items (item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_id_1) REFERENCES items (item_id)
);

CREATE TABLE reaction_times (
  rt_id INTEGER ,
  trial_id INTEGER ,
  reaction_time INTEGER ,
  reaction_time_variance REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rt_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (trial_id) REFERENCES trials (trial_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  institution TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  job_title TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE leagues (
  league_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (league_id)
);

  season_id INTEGER ,
  season_name TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (season_id)
);

  match_type_id INTEGER ,
  match_type_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (match_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE teams (
  team_id INTEGER ,
  team_name TEXT ,
  league_id INTEGER ,
  season_id INTEGER ,
  points INTEGER ,
  wins INTEGER ,
  losses INTEGER ,
  draws INTEGER ,
  goals_for INTEGER ,
  goals_against INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (team_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (league_id) REFERENCES leagues (league_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (season_id) REFERENCES seasons (season_id)
);

CREATE TABLE matches (
  match_id INTEGER ,
  date TEXT ,
  home_team_id INTEGER ,
  away_team_id INTEGER ,
  home_goals INTEGER ,
  away_goals INTEGER ,
  match_result TEXT ,
  home_shots INTEGER ,
  away_shots INTEGER ,
  home_corners INTEGER ,
  away_corners INTEGER ,
  home_possession REAL ,
  away_possession REAL ,
  match_type_id INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (match_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (home_team_id) REFERENCES teams (team_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (away_team_id) REFERENCES teams (team_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (match_type_id) REFERENCES match_types (match_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE players (
  player_id INTEGER ,
  player_name TEXT ,
  team_id INTEGER ,
  position TEXT ,
  goals_scored INTEGER ,
  assists INTEGER ,
  yellow_cards INTEGER ,
  red_cards INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (player_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams (team_id)
);

CREATE TABLE match_statistics (
  statistic_id INTEGER ,
  match_id INTEGER ,
  home_team_long_passes INTEGER ,
  away_team_long_passes INTEGER ,
  home_team_short_passes INTEGER ,
  away_team_short_passes INTEGER ,
  home_team_shots_on_target INTEGER ,
  away_team_shots_on_target INTEGER ,
  home_team_shots_off_target INTEGER ,
  away_team_shots_off_target INTEGER ,
  home_team_corners_taken INTEGER ,
  away_team_corners_taken INTEGER ,
  home_team_offsides INTEGER ,
  away_team_offsides INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (statistic_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES matches (match_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (

  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

  user_id INTEGER ,

  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES user_roles (role_id)
);

  department TEXT ,

-- Database: housing_and_support_services_data_management

CREATE TABLE movements (
  movement_id INTEGER ,
  exit_destination_id INTEGER ,
  population_type TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  question_id INTEGER ,
  category_id INTEGER ,
  household_type_id INTEGER ,
  total INTEGER ,
  count INTEGER ,
  program_id INTEGER ,
  household_id INTEGER ,
  individual_id INTEGER ,

  updated_at TEXT ,

  updated_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (movement_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (exit_destination_id) REFERENCES exit_destinations (exit_destination_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (question_id) REFERENCES questions (question_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES destination_categories (category_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (household_type_id) REFERENCES household_types (household_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (household_id) REFERENCES households (household_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)

  PRIMARY KEY (category_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_category_id) REFERENCES destination_categories (category_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)

  is_active INTEGER ,
  helpful_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (review_id) REFERENCES reviews (review_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE forums (
  forum_id INTEGER ,
  title TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  member_count INTEGER ,
  thread_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (forum_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  thread_id INTEGER ,
  forum_id INTEGER ,
  title TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  post_count INTEGER ,
  view_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (thread_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (forum_id) REFERENCES forums (forum_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE posts (
  post_id INTEGER ,
  thread_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  post_date TEXT ,
  post_text TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  like_count INTEGER ,
  dislike_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (post_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (thread_id) REFERENCES threads (thread_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tags (
  tag_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  use_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tag_id)
);

CREATE TABLE taggings (
  tagging_id INTEGER ,
  tag_id INTEGER ,
  content_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  tagging_date TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tagging_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tag_id) REFERENCES tags (tag_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (content_id) REFERENCES content (content_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: loan_portfolio_analysis_and_risk_management

CREATE TABLE loan_segments (
  segment_id INTEGER ,
  credit_score_range TEXT ,
  total_loans INTEGER ,
  percentage_of_loans REAL ,
  total_defaults INTEGER ,
  percentage_of_defaults REAL ,
  segment_description TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  updated_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (segment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE loans (
  loan_id INTEGER ,
  borrower_id INTEGER ,
  loan_type_id INTEGER ,
  loan_amount REAL ,
  interest_rate REAL ,
  credit_score INTEGER ,
  segment_id INTEGER ,
  loan_status TEXT ,
  origination_date TEXT ,
  maturity_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (loan_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (borrower_id) REFERENCES borrowers (borrower_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (loan_type_id) REFERENCES loan_types (loan_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (segment_id) REFERENCES loan_segments (segment_id)

  test_subcategory_id INTEGER ,
  test_id INTEGER ,
  subcategory_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (test_subcategory_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES tests (test_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (subcategory_id) REFERENCES test_subcategories (subcategory_id)
);

  macro_recall REAL ,
  macro_f1_score REAL ,

);

  resolved INTEGER ,

);

-- Database: numerical_methods_and_computational_algorithms_for_matrix_factorization

CREATE TABLE solvers (
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  solver_name TEXT ,
  factorization_method TEXT ,
  supports_indefinite INTEGER ,
  out_of_core INTEGER ,
  parallelisation TEXT ,
  solver_description TEXT ,
  default_version TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (solver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE factorization_methods (
  factorization_id INTEGER ,
  method_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  is_parallelizable INTEGER ,
  is_out_of_core INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (factorization_id)
);

CREATE TABLE solver_settings (
  setting_id INTEGER ,
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  setting_name TEXT ,
  setting_value TEXT ,
  is_required INTEGER ,
  default_value TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (setting_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (solver_id) REFERENCES solvers (solver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE performance_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  execution_time REAL ,
  memory_usage REAL ,
  date_logged TEXT ,
  solver_version TEXT ,
  input_size INTEGER ,
  error_rate REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (solver_id) REFERENCES solvers (solver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (role) REFERENCES user_roles (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (solver_id) REFERENCES solvers (solver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE versions (
  version_id INTEGER ,
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  version_number TEXT ,
  release_date TEXT ,
  change_log TEXT ,
  is_stable INTEGER ,
  deprecation_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (version_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (solver_id) REFERENCES solvers (solver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (
  role_id INTEGER ,
  role_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  permissions TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE solver_parameters (
  parameter_id INTEGER ,
  solver_id INTEGER ,
  parameter_name TEXT ,
  parameter_value TEXT ,
  is_required INTEGER ,
  default_value TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (parameter_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (solver_id) REFERENCES solvers (solver_id)
);

-- Database: pathfinding_and_terrain_analysis_in_virtual_environments

CREATE TABLE maps (
  map_id TEXT ,

  terrain_type TEXT ,

  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  map_size REAL ,
  complexity INTEGER ,
  author_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (map_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (author_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE scenarios (
  scen_id INTEGER ,
  scen_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  map_id TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  difficulty_level TEXT ,
  author_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (scen_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (map_id) REFERENCES maps (map_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (author_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pathfind_results (
  result_id INTEGER ,
  scen_id INTEGER ,
  tcost REAL ,
  distance REAL ,
  expect REAL ,
  steps INTEGER ,
  d INTEGER ,
  itype TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  execution_time REAL ,
  algorithm_version TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (result_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (scen_id) REFERENCES scenarios (scen_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (itype) REFERENCES iterations (itype_id)
);

CREATE TABLE iterations (
  itype_id TEXT ,
  itype_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  optimization_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (itype_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  result_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (result_id) REFERENCES pathfind_results (result_id)
);

-- Database: real_time_data_monitoring_and_control_systems

CREATE TABLE sensor_data (
  data_id INTEGER ,
  time REAL ,
  y_sum INTEGER ,
  y_product INTEGER ,
  y_triggeredAdd INTEGER ,
  y_multiSwitch INTEGER ,
  sensor_id INTEGER ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  machine_id INTEGER ,
  event_id INTEGER ,
  data_source TEXT ,
  data_quality TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (data_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sensor_id) REFERENCES sensors (sensor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (machine_id) REFERENCES equipment (equipment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (event_id) REFERENCES events (event_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sensors (
  sensor_id INTEGER ,
  sensor_name TEXT ,
  sensor_type TEXT ,
  installation_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  machine_id INTEGER ,
  calibration_date TEXT ,
  calibration_due TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sensor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (machine_id) REFERENCES equipment (equipment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE alerts (
  alert_id INTEGER ,
  alert_type TEXT ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  sensor_id INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  message TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  assigned_to INTEGER ,
  resolution_time TEXT ,
  resolution_details TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (alert_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sensor_id) REFERENCES sensors (sensor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (assigned_to) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE roles (
  role_id INTEGER ,
  role_name TEXT ,
  permissions TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE data_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  data_id INTEGER ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  log_message TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  machine_id INTEGER ,
  log_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id)
);

CREATE TABLE equipment (
  equipment_id INTEGER ,
  equipment_name TEXT ,
  equipment_type TEXT ,
  installation_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  model TEXT ,
  manufacturer TEXT ,
  warranty_expiration TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (equipment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE maintenance_records (
  maintenance_id INTEGER ,
  equipment_id INTEGER ,
  maintenance_date TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  performed_by INTEGER ,
  parts_used TEXT ,
  cost TEXT ,
  next_maintenance_due TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (maintenance_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (equipment_id) REFERENCES equipment (equipment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  notification_id INTEGER ,
  alert_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  read_at TEXT ,
  read_status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (notification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (alert_id) REFERENCES alerts (alert_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE events (
  event_id INTEGER ,
  event_type TEXT ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  sensor_id INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  machine_id INTEGER ,
  priority TEXT ,
  impact TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (event_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sensor_id) REFERENCES sensors (sensor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (machine_id) REFERENCES equipment (equipment_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES work_categories (category_id)

-- Database: software_project_management_and_issue_tracking_344821

CREATE TABLE tickets (
  ticket_id INTEGER ,
  summary TEXT ,
  status_id INTEGER ,
  type_id INTEGER ,
  priority_id INTEGER ,
  milestone_id INTEGER ,
  component_id INTEGER ,
  reporter_id INTEGER ,
  assignee_id INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (ticket_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (status_id) REFERENCES statuses (status_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (type_id) REFERENCES ticket_types (type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (priority_id) REFERENCES priorities (priority_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (milestone_id) REFERENCES milestones (milestone_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (component_id) REFERENCES components (component_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (reporter_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (assignee_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE milestones (
  milestone_id INTEGER ,
  milestone_name TEXT ,
  due_date TEXT ,
  project_id INTEGER ,
  description TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (milestone_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (project_id) REFERENCES projects (project_id)
);

  component_id INTEGER ,
  component_name TEXT ,
  project_id INTEGER ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (component_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (project_id) REFERENCES projects (project_id)

  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  project_manager_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (project_id)
);

  status_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (status_id)
);

  type_id INTEGER ,

);

  user_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ticket_id) REFERENCES tickets (ticket_id)

  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

-- Database: statistical_analysis_of_event_rates_in_populations

  bin_id INTEGER ,

  event_count INTEGER ,
  non_event_count INTEGER ,

  cumulative_event_pct REAL ,

  PRIMARY KEY (bin_id)

  median REAL ,
  std_dev REAL ,
  variance REAL ,

  max_value REAL ,
  quartiles TEXT ,
  skewness REAL ,
  kurtosis REAL ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (statistic_id),

  user_id INTEGER ,
  notification_text TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  is_read INTEGER ,
  notification_type TEXT ,
  priority TEXT ,
  expires_at TEXT ,
  sent_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (notification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES user_profiles (user_id)
);

-- Database: stock_market_and_company_performance_analysis_30702

CREATE TABLE companies (
  company_id INTEGER ,
  company_name TEXT ,
  sector_id INTEGER ,
  current_market_price REAL ,
  change_percentage REAL ,
  volume REAL ,
  technical_rating_id INTEGER ,
  company_description TEXT ,
  founded_year INTEGER ,
  ceo_name TEXT ,
  headquarters TEXT ,
  website TEXT ,
  stock_exchange TEXT ,
  ticker_symbol TEXT ,
  market_capitalization REAL ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (company_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sector_id) REFERENCES sectors (sector_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (technical_rating_id) REFERENCES technical_ratings (rating_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sectors (
  sector_id INTEGER ,
  sector_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  parent_sector_id INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sector_id)
);

  data_id INTEGER ,
  company_id INTEGER ,
  date TEXT ,
  current_market_price REAL ,
  change_percentage REAL ,
  volume REAL ,
  open_price REAL ,
  close_price REAL ,
  high_price REAL ,
  low_price REAL ,
  adjusted_close_price REAL ,
  dividend_amount REAL ,
  split_coefficient REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (data_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES companies (company_id)
);

CREATE TABLE technical_ratings (
  rating_id INTEGER ,
  rating_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  source TEXT ,
  date_generated TEXT ,
  valid_until TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rating_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,

  last_login TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,

  access_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  company_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  is_successful INTEGER ,

  FOREIGN KEY (company_id) REFERENCES companies (company_id)
);

CREATE TABLE survey_responses (
  response_id INTEGER ,
  participant_id INTEGER ,
  variable_id INTEGER ,
  comparison_id INTEGER ,
  physical REAL ,
  emotional REAL ,
  social REAL ,
  school REAL ,
  psychosocial REAL ,
  total REAL ,
  response_date TEXT ,
  program_id INTEGER ,
  additional_comments TEXT ,

  FOREIGN KEY (participant_id) REFERENCES participants (participant_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (variable_id) REFERENCES variables (variable_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comparison_id) REFERENCES comparisons (comparison_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (program_id) REFERENCES prevention_programs (program_id)

CREATE TABLE presidential_orders (
  order_id INTEGER ,
  president_id INTEGER ,
  order_name TEXT ,
  order_date TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (order_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (president_id) REFERENCES presidents (president_id)
);

CREATE TABLE speeches (
  speech_id INTEGER ,
  president_id INTEGER ,
  speech_name TEXT ,
  speech_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (speech_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (president_id) REFERENCES presidents (president_id)
);

CREATE TABLE treaties (
  treaty_id INTEGER ,
  president_id INTEGER ,
  treaty_name TEXT ,
  treaty_date TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (treaty_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (president_id) REFERENCES presidents (president_id)
);

CREATE TABLE legislation (
  legislation_id INTEGER ,
  president_id INTEGER ,
  legislation_name TEXT ,
  legislation_date TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (legislation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (president_id) REFERENCES presidents (president_id)
);

CREATE TABLE appointments (
  appointment_id INTEGER ,
  president_id INTEGER ,
  appointment_name TEXT ,
  appointment_date TEXT ,
  position TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (appointment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (president_id) REFERENCES presidents (president_id)
);

-- Database: vaccination_status_and_demographics_by_district_health_board__dhb_

CREATE TABLE district_health_boards (
  dhb_id INTEGER ,
  dhb_name TEXT ,
  region TEXT ,
  dhb_email TEXT ,
  dhb_phone TEXT ,
  dhb_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dhb_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ethnic_groups (
  ethnic_group_id INTEGER ,
  ethnic_group_name TEXT ,
  ethnic_group_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (ethnic_group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE age_groups (
  age_group_id INTEGER ,
  age_group_range TEXT ,
  age_group_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (age_group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE genders (
  gender_id INTEGER ,
  gender_name TEXT ,
  gender_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (gender_id)
);

CREATE TABLE vaccination_types (
  vaccination_type_id INTEGER ,
  vaccination_type_name TEXT ,
  vaccination_type_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (vaccination_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE vaccination_stats (
  stat_id INTEGER ,
  dhb_id INTEGER ,
  ethnic_group_id INTEGER ,
  age_group_id INTEGER ,
  gender_id INTEGER ,
  partially_vaccinated INTEGER ,
  fully_vaccinated INTEGER ,
  eligible_for_booster INTEGER ,
  booster_received INTEGER ,
  population INTEGER ,
  reporting_date TEXT ,
  data_source TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (stat_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dhb_id) REFERENCES district_health_boards (dhb_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ethnic_group_id) REFERENCES ethnic_groups (ethnic_group_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (age_group_id) REFERENCES age_groups (age_group_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gender_id) REFERENCES genders (gender_id)
);

CREATE TABLE booster_eligibility (
  eligibility_id INTEGER ,
  eligibility_criteria TEXT ,
  eligibility_start_date TEXT ,
  eligibility_end_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (eligibility_id)
);

CREATE TABLE booster_administration (
  administration_id INTEGER ,
  stat_id INTEGER ,
  administration_date TEXT ,
  vaccination_type_id INTEGER ,
  administration_method TEXT ,
  administration_location TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (administration_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (stat_id) REFERENCES vaccination_stats (stat_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (vaccination_type_id) REFERENCES vaccination_types (vaccination_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE population_data (
  population_id INTEGER ,
  dhb_id INTEGER ,
  ethnic_group_id INTEGER ,
  age_group_id INTEGER ,
  gender_id INTEGER ,
  population_count INTEGER ,
  population_source TEXT ,
  population_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (population_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dhb_id) REFERENCES district_health_boards (dhb_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ethnic_group_id) REFERENCES ethnic_groups (ethnic_group_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (age_group_id) REFERENCES age_groups (age_group_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gender_id) REFERENCES genders (gender_id)
);

CREATE TABLE vaccination_campaigns (
  campaign_id INTEGER ,
  campaign_name TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  target_population TEXT ,
  campaign_budget REAL ,
  campaign_manager TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (campaign_id)
);

CREATE TABLE campaign_outreach (
  outreach_id INTEGER ,
  campaign_id INTEGER ,
  outreach_method TEXT ,
  outreach_date TEXT ,
  reach_count INTEGER ,
  outreach_budget REAL ,
  outreach_staff INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (outreach_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (campaign_id) REFERENCES vaccination_campaigns (campaign_id)
);

CREATE TABLE campaign_impact (
  impact_id INTEGER ,
  campaign_id INTEGER ,
  impact_metric TEXT ,
  impact_value REAL ,
  measurement_date TEXT ,
  impact_confidence_interval REAL ,
  impact_margin_of_error REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (impact_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (campaign_id) REFERENCES vaccination_campaigns (campaign_id)
);

CREATE TABLE security_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  event_type TEXT ,
  event_description TEXT ,
  event_date TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  event_severity TEXT ,
  event_resolution TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  user_password TEXT ,
  user_last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

-- Database: vehicle_sales_and_listings_management

CREATE TABLE vehicles (
  vehicle_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  make INTEGER ,
  model INTEGER ,
  trim INTEGER ,
  mileage INTEGER ,
  price REAL ,
  transmission INTEGER ,
  wheel_drive INTEGER ,
  fuel_type INTEGER ,
  body_type INTEGER ,
  color INTEGER ,
  location INTEGER ,
  VIN TEXT ,
  engine_size REAL ,
  interior_color TEXT ,
  exterior_features TEXT ,
  interior_features TEXT ,
  is_sold INTEGER ,
  date_listed TEXT ,
  date_sold TEXT ,
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (vehicle_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (make) REFERENCES makes (make_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (model) REFERENCES models (model_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (trim) REFERENCES trims (trim_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (transmission) REFERENCES transmissions (transmission_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (wheel_drive) REFERENCES wheel_drives (wheel_drive_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (fuel_type) REFERENCES fuel_types (fuel_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (body_type) REFERENCES body_types (body_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (color) REFERENCES colors (color_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (location) REFERENCES locations (location_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (condition_id) REFERENCES conditions (condition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE makes (
  make_id INTEGER ,
  make_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (make_id)
);

CREATE TABLE models (
  model_id INTEGER ,
  model_name TEXT ,
  make_id INTEGER ,
  seating_capacity INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (model_id)
);

CREATE TABLE trims (
  trim_id INTEGER ,
  trim_name TEXT ,
  model_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (trim_id)
);

CREATE TABLE locations (
  location_id INTEGER ,
  location_name TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  contact_number TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (location_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transmissions (
  transmission_id INTEGER ,
  transmission_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transmission_id)
);

CREATE TABLE wheel_drives (
  wheel_drive_id INTEGER ,
  wheel_drive_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (wheel_drive_id)
);

CREATE TABLE fuel_types (
  fuel_type_id INTEGER ,
  fuel_type_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (fuel_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE body_types (
  body_type_id INTEGER ,
  body_type_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (body_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE colors (
  color_id INTEGER ,
  color_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (color_id)
);

CREATE TABLE websites (
  website_id INTEGER ,
  website_name TEXT ,
  url TEXT ,
  active INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (website_id)
);

CREATE TABLE listings (
  listing_id INTEGER ,
  vehicle_id INTEGER ,
  website_id INTEGER ,
  title TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  price REAL ,
  url TEXT ,
  date_posted TEXT ,
  date_removed TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (listing_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (vehicle_id) REFERENCES vehicles (vehicle_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (website_id) REFERENCES websites (website_id)
);

CREATE TABLE conditions (
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  condition_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (condition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE vehicles_conditions (
  vehicle_id INTEGER ,
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (vehicle_id, condition_id)
);

-- Database: video_comment_analysis_and_sentiment_tracking

CREATE TABLE videos (
  video_id TEXT ,
  video_title TEXT ,
  video_description TEXT ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  uploader_id INTEGER ,
  video_url TEXT ,
  thumbnail_url TEXT ,
  duration TEXT ,
  views INTEGER ,
  likes INTEGER ,
  dislikes INTEGER ,
  category TEXT ,
  tags TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (video_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (uploader_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  registration_date TEXT ,
  last_active_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  language TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comments (
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  video_id TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  comment_text TEXT ,
  comment_date TEXT ,
  likes INTEGER ,
  sentiment INTEGER ,
  parent_comment_id INTEGER ,
  edited INTEGER ,
  report_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (video_id) REFERENCES videos (video_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sentiment) REFERENCES sentiment_categories (sentiment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE replies (
  reply_id INTEGER ,
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  reply_text TEXT ,
  reply_date TEXT ,
  likes INTEGER ,
  sentiment INTEGER ,
  edited INTEGER ,
  report_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (reply_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comment_id) REFERENCES comments (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sentiment) REFERENCES sentiment_categories (sentiment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sentiment_categories (
  sentiment_id INTEGER ,
  sentiment_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sentiment_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (
  role_id INTEGER ,
  role_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comment_versions (
  version_id INTEGER ,
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  version_text TEXT ,
  version_date TEXT ,
  version_user_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (version_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comment_id) REFERENCES comments (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (version_user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE video_views (
  view_id INTEGER ,
  video_id TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  view_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  device TEXT ,
  platform TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (view_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (video_id) REFERENCES videos (video_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE video_likes (
  like_id INTEGER ,
  video_id TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  like_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (like_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (video_id) REFERENCES videos (video_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE video_dislikes (
  dislike_id INTEGER ,
  video_id TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  dislike_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dislike_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (video_id) REFERENCES videos (video_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE reports (
  report_id INTEGER ,
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  reply_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  report_date TEXT ,
  reason TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (report_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (comment_id) REFERENCES comments (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (reply_id) REFERENCES replies (reply_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: video_game_weapon_statistics_and_skills

CREATE TABLE weapons (
  weapon_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  physical_damage INTEGER ,
  magical_damage INTEGER ,
  fire_damage INTEGER ,
  lightning_damage INTEGER ,
  holy_damage INTEGER ,
  critical_boost INTEGER ,
  strength_requirement TEXT ,
  dexterity_requirement TEXT ,
  intelligence_requirement TEXT ,
  faith_requirement TEXT ,
  arcane_requirement TEXT ,
  weight INTEGER ,
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  rarity TEXT ,
  obtain_method TEXT ,
  durability INTEGER ,
  crafting_materials TEXT ,
  icon_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (weapon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE skills (
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  skill_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  damage_type TEXT ,
  cooldown INTEGER ,
  cost TEXT ,
  effect_duration INTEGER ,
  range INTEGER ,
  target_type TEXT ,
  effect_description TEXT ,
  icon_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE damage_types (
  damage_type_id INTEGER ,
  damage_type_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  resistance_multiplier TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (damage_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE attributes (
  attribute_id INTEGER ,
  attribute_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  icon_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (attribute_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weapon_attributes (
  weapon_id INTEGER ,
  attribute_id INTEGER ,
  attribute_value TEXT ,
  attribute_multiplier TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (weapon_id, attribute_id)
);

CREATE TABLE characters (
  character_id INTEGER ,
  character_name TEXT ,
  level INTEGER ,
  strength INTEGER ,
  dexterity INTEGER ,
  intelligence INTEGER ,
  faith INTEGER ,
  arcane INTEGER ,
  max_health INTEGER ,
  current_health INTEGER ,
  max_stamina INTEGER ,
  current_stamina INTEGER ,
  max_mana INTEGER ,
  current_mana INTEGER ,
  icon_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (character_id)
);

CREATE TABLE character_equipment (
  character_id INTEGER ,
  weapon_id INTEGER ,
  slot TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (character_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (weapon_id) REFERENCES weapons (weapon_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weapon_categories (
  category_id INTEGER ,
  category_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  icon_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weapon_category_mapping (
  weapon_id INTEGER ,
  category_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (weapon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES weapon_categories (category_id)
);

-- Database: wearable_health_and_movement_tracking

CREATE TABLE sessions (
  session_id TEXT ,
  logging_time TEXT ,
  user_id TEXT ,
  session_duration INTEGER ,
  device_id TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  session_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE activity_logs (
  rec_no INTEGER ,
  session_id TEXT ,
  hertz INTEGER ,
  activity TEXT ,
  move_type TEXT ,
  calories_burned REAL ,
  heart_rate INTEGER ,
  activity_duration INTEGER ,
  activity_intensity TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rec_no), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_profiles (
  user_id TEXT ,
  name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  age INTEGER ,
  gender TEXT ,
  height REAL ,
  weight REAL ,
  fitness_goal TEXT ,
  activity_level TEXT ,
  medical_conditions TEXT ,
  allergies TEXT ,
  preferred_language TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE movement_data (
  timestamp TEXT ,
  session_id TEXT ,
  dm_roll REAL ,
  dm_pitch REAL ,
  dm_yaw REAL ,
  dm_rot_x REAL ,
  dm_rot_y REAL ,
  dm_rot_z REAL ,
  dm_speed REAL ,
  dm_distance REAL ,
  dm_elevation_gain REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (timestamp), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE acceleration_data (
  timestamp TEXT ,
  session_id TEXT ,
  dm_grv_x REAL ,
  dm_grv_y REAL ,
  dm_grv_z REAL ,
  dm_u_accel_x REAL ,
  dm_u_accel_y REAL ,
  dm_u_accel_z REAL ,
  dm_jerk_x REAL ,
  dm_jerk_y REAL ,
  dm_jerk_z REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (timestamp), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE quaternion_data (
  timestamp TEXT ,
  session_id TEXT ,
  dm_quat_x REAL ,
  dm_quat_y REAL ,
  dm_quat_w REAL ,
  dm_quat_z REAL ,
  dm_angular_velocity_x REAL ,
  dm_angular_velocity_y REAL ,
  dm_angular_velocity_z REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (timestamp), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (session_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id TEXT ,
  session_id TEXT ,
  access_time TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  device_type TEXT ,
  access_location TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES user_profiles (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES sessions (session_id)
);

-- Database: weather_data_collection_and_analysis_394636

CREATE TABLE weather_reports (
  report_id INTEGER ,
  source_id INTEGER ,
  location_id INTEGER ,
  report_time TEXT ,
  dewpoint REAL ,
  wdir INTEGER ,
  qpf REAL ,
  pop INTEGER ,
  mslp REAL ,
  temp REAL ,
  sky INTEGER ,
  snow REAL ,
  humidity REAL ,
  wspd REAL ,
  visibility REAL ,
  uv_index INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (report_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (source_id) REFERENCES weather_sources (source_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (location_id) REFERENCES locations (location_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weather_sources (
  source_id INTEGER ,
  source_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  url TEXT ,
  contact_info TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (source_id)
);

CREATE TABLE conditions (
  condition_id INTEGER ,
  condition_description TEXT ,
  severity_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (condition_id)
);

  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,

  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_time TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (report_id) REFERENCES weather_reports (report_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (report_id) REFERENCES weather_reports (report_id)

CREATE TABLE locations (

  state TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,

  longitude REAL ,

  PRIMARY KEY (location_id)
);

CREATE TABLE weather_conditions (
  weather_condition_id INTEGER ,

  FOREIGN KEY (report_id) REFERENCES weather_reports (report_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (condition_id) REFERENCES conditions (condition_id)

-- Database: world_happiness_report_data

CREATE TABLE countries (
  country_id INTEGER ,
  country_name TEXT ,
  region TEXT ,
  population INTEGER ,
  gdp_total REAL ,
  gdp_growth_rate REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE happiness_scores (
  score_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  happiness_rank INTEGER ,
  happiness_score REAL ,
  standard_error REAL ,
  year INTEGER ,
  source TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (score_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE economy (
  economy_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  gdp_per_capita REAL ,
  inflation_rate REAL ,
  unemployment_rate REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (economy_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE family_life (
  family_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  family_score REAL ,
  divorce_rate REAL ,
  average_family_size REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (family_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE health (
  health_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  life_expectancy REAL ,
  infant_mortality_rate REAL ,
  healthcare_expenditure REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (health_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)

CREATE TABLE freedom (

  PRIMARY KEY (freedom_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE trust (
  trust_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  corruption_score REAL ,
  government_effectiveness REAL ,
  rule_of_law REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (trust_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE generosity (
  generosity_id INTEGER ,
  country_id INTEGER ,
  generosity_score REAL ,
  charitable_donations REAL ,
  volunteer_rate REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (generosity_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dystopia (

  poverty_rate REAL ,
  income_inequality REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dystopia_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (country_id) REFERENCES countries (country_id)
