-- Database: node_transition_probability_and_value_analysis

CREATE TABLE nodes (
  node_id INTEGER ,
  node_name TEXT ,
  node_type TEXT ,
  node_description TEXT ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (node_id)
);

CREATE TABLE edges (
  edge_id INTEGER ,
  edge_name TEXT ,
  source_node_id INTEGER ,
  target_node_id INTEGER ,
  edge_type TEXT ,
  weight REAL ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (edge_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (source_node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (target_node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transitions (
  transition_id INTEGER ,
  edge_id INTEGER ,
  old_node_id INTEGER ,
  current_node_id INTEGER ,
  time INTEGER ,
  probability REAL ,
  transition_type TEXT ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transition_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (edge_id) REFERENCES edges (edge_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (old_node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (current_node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transition_values (
  transition_value_id INTEGER ,
  transition_id INTEGER ,
  value_func TEXT ,
  expected_value REAL ,
  actual_value REAL ,
  difference REAL ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transition_value_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (transition_id) REFERENCES transitions (transition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE node_neighbors (
  node_neighbor_id INTEGER ,
  node_id INTEGER ,
  neighbor_node_id INTEGER ,
  edge_id INTEGER ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (node_neighbor_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (neighbor_node_id) REFERENCES nodes (node_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (edge_id) REFERENCES edges (edge_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password TEXT ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transition_probabilities (
  probability_id INTEGER ,
  transition_id INTEGER ,
  probability REAL ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (probability_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (transition_id) REFERENCES transitions (transition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE node_transition_counts (
  node_transition_count_id INTEGER ,
  node_id INTEGER ,
  transition_count INTEGER ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (node_transition_count_id)
);

CREATE TABLE edge_transition_counts (
  edge_transition_count_id INTEGER ,
  edge_id INTEGER ,
  transition_count INTEGER ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (edge_transition_count_id)
);

CREATE TABLE system_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  log_message TEXT ,
  log_time TEXT ,
  log_level TEXT ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transition_value_history (
  transition_value_history_id INTEGER ,
  transition_value_id INTEGER ,
  transition_id INTEGER ,
  value_func TEXT ,
  expected_value REAL ,
  actual_value REAL ,
  difference REAL ,
  creation_time TEXT ,
  update_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transition_value_history_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (transition_value_id) REFERENCES transition_values (transition_value_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (transition_id) REFERENCES transitions (transition_id)
);

-- Database: aerial_phenomenon_and_ufo_sightings

CREATE TABLE sightings (
  sighting_id INTEGER ,
  datetime TEXT ,
  city TEXT ,
  state TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  shape TEXT ,
  duration_seconds INTEGER ,
  duration_hours_minutes TEXT ,
  comments TEXT ,
  date_posted TEXT ,
  latitude REAL ,
  longitude REAL ,
  year INTEGER ,
  month TEXT ,
  day_of_month INTEGER ,
  day_of_week TEXT ,
  hour INTEGER ,
  weekend INTEGER ,
  decade INTEGER ,
  us_holiday TEXT ,
  reporter_id INTEGER ,
  verified INTEGER ,
  verification_date TEXT ,
  verification_comments TEXT ,
  source TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (sighting_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (city) REFERENCES cities (city_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (shape) REFERENCES shapes (shape_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (duration_seconds) REFERENCES durations (duration_seconds), 
  FOREIGN KEY (reporter_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE cities (
  city_id INTEGER ,
  city_name TEXT ,
  state TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  population INTEGER ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (city_id)
);

CREATE TABLE shapes (
  shape_id INTEGER ,
  shape_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (shape_id)
);

CREATE TABLE durations (
  duration_id INTEGER ,
  duration_seconds INTEGER ,
  duration_hours_minutes TEXT ,
  duration_minutes INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (duration_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comments (
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  sighting_id INTEGER ,
  comment_text TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  comment_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sighting_id) REFERENCES sightings (sighting_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE locations (
  location_id INTEGER ,
  latitude REAL ,
  longitude REAL ,
  city_id INTEGER ,
  address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (location_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (latitude) REFERENCES sightings (latitude), 
  FOREIGN KEY (city_id) REFERENCES cities (city_id)
);

CREATE TABLE years (
  year_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  century TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (year_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (year) REFERENCES sightings (year)
);

CREATE TABLE months (
  month_id INTEGER ,
  month TEXT ,
  season TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (month_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (month) REFERENCES sightings (month)
);

CREATE TABLE days_of_month (
  day_of_month_id INTEGER ,

  week_number INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (day_of_month_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (day_of_month) REFERENCES sightings (day_of_month)
);

CREATE TABLE days_of_week (
  day_of_week_id INTEGER ,
  day_of_week TEXT ,
  holiday TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (day_of_week_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (day_of_week) REFERENCES sightings (day_of_week)
);

CREATE TABLE hours (
  hour_id INTEGER ,
  hour INTEGER ,
  part_of_day TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (hour_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (hour) REFERENCES sightings (hour)
);

CREATE TABLE weekends (
  weekend_id INTEGER ,
  weekend INTEGER ,
  week_number INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (weekend_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (weekend) REFERENCES sightings (weekend)
);

CREATE TABLE decades (
  decade_id INTEGER ,
  decade INTEGER ,
  century TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (decade_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (decade) REFERENCES sightings (decade)
);

CREATE TABLE us_holidays (
  us_holiday_id INTEGER ,
  us_holiday TEXT ,
  holiday_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (us_holiday_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (us_holiday) REFERENCES sightings (us_holiday)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  phone TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,

  ip_address TEXT ,
  device_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id),

  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: web_server_and_tls_certificate_information

CREATE TABLE webservers (
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  input TEXT ,
  title TEXT ,
  status_code INTEGER ,
  content_length INTEGER ,
  tls_host TEXT ,
  tls_port INTEGER ,
  tls_certificate_status TEXT ,
  last_scan_date TEXT ,
  scanned_by TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (webserver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tls_certificates (
  tls_certificate_id INTEGER ,
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  tls_probe_status INTEGER ,
  tls_tls_version TEXT ,
  tls_cipher TEXT ,
  tls_not_before TEXT ,
  tls_not_after TEXT ,
  certificate_type TEXT ,
  certificate_authority TEXT ,
  certificate_serial_number TEXT ,
  is_self_signed INTEGER ,
  is_expired INTEGER ,
  version INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tls_certificate_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (webserver_id) REFERENCES webservers (webserver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tls_subjects (
  tls_subject_id INTEGER ,
  tls_certificate_id INTEGER ,
  tls_subject_dn TEXT ,
  tls_subject_cn TEXT ,
  tls_subject_org TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  state TEXT ,
  locality TEXT ,
  email_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tls_subject_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tls_certificate_id) REFERENCES tls_certificates (tls_certificate_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tls_issuers (
  tls_issuer_id INTEGER ,
  tls_certificate_id INTEGER ,
  tls_issuer_dn TEXT ,
  tls_issuer_cn TEXT ,
  tls_issuer_org TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  state TEXT ,
  locality TEXT ,
  email_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tls_issuer_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tls_certificate_id) REFERENCES tls_certificates (tls_certificate_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tls_fingerprints (
  tls_fingerprint_id INTEGER ,
  tls_certificate_id INTEGER ,
  tls_fingerprint_hash_md TEXT ,
  tls_fingerprint_hash_sha TEXT ,
  tls_fingerprint_hash_sha256 TEXT ,
  tls_fingerprint_hash_sha512 TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tls_fingerprint_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tls_certificate_id) REFERENCES tls_certificates (tls_certificate_id)
);

CREATE TABLE tls_connections (
  tls_connection_id INTEGER ,
  tls_certificate_id INTEGER ,
  tls_tls_connection TEXT ,
  tls_sni TEXT ,
  tls_connection_status TEXT ,
  tls_connection_time INTEGER ,
  tls_server_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tls_connection_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tls_certificate_id) REFERENCES tls_certificates (tls_certificate_id)
);

CREATE TABLE urls (
  url_id INTEGER ,
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  url TEXT ,
  scheme TEXT ,
  port INTEGER ,
  last_visited_date TEXT ,
  response_time INTEGER ,
  response_headers TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (url_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (webserver_id) REFERENCES webservers (webserver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE webservers_technologies (
  webservers_technology_id INTEGER ,
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  technology TEXT ,
  technology_version TEXT ,
  is_enabled INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (webservers_technology_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (webserver_id) REFERENCES webservers (webserver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_log_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  response_time INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (webserver_id) REFERENCES webservers (webserver_id)
);

CREATE TABLE favicon_hashes (
  favicon_hash_id INTEGER ,
  webserver_id INTEGER ,
  favicon_hash TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (favicon_hash_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (webserver_id) REFERENCES webservers (webserver_id)
);

-- Database: broadcast_licensing_and_regulatory_compliance

CREATE TABLE licenses (
  license_id INTEGER ,
  file_number TEXT ,
  purpose TEXT ,
  service_type TEXT ,
  call_sign TEXT ,
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  station_type TEXT ,
  channel_frequency TEXT ,
  city_state TEXT ,
  applicant_name TEXT ,
  status_date TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  application_type TEXT ,
  fees_paid TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (license_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (service_type) REFERENCES services (service_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (facility_id) REFERENCES facilities (facility_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (applicant_name) REFERENCES applicants (applicant_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (status) REFERENCES statuses (status_id)
);

CREATE TABLE applicants (
  applicant_id INTEGER ,
  applicant_name TEXT ,
  contact_info TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  representative_name TEXT ,
  representative_email TEXT ,
  representative_phone TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (applicant_id)

);

CREATE TABLE stations (
  station_id INTEGER ,

  service_id INTEGER ,

  channel_frequency TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  latitude TEXT ,
  longitude TEXT ,

);

CREATE TABLE statuses (

CREATE TABLE license_history (

CREATE TABLE contacts (
  contact_id INTEGER ,

  phone TEXT ,

  PRIMARY KEY (contact_id)
);

CREATE TABLE application_types (
  application_type_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (application_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE review_logs (
  review_id INTEGER ,

  review_status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (review_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (license_id) REFERENCES licenses (license_id)
);

CREATE TABLE facilities (
  facility_id INTEGER ,
  facility_name TEXT ,
  facility_type TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  equipment_list TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (facility_id)
);

CREATE TABLE fees (
  fee_id INTEGER ,
  license_id INTEGER ,

  payment_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (fee_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (license_id) REFERENCES licenses (license_id)
);

CREATE TABLE communications (
  communication_id INTEGER ,
  license_id INTEGER ,
  communication_date TEXT ,
  communication_type TEXT ,
  content TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (communication_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (license_id) REFERENCES licenses (license_id)
);

-- Database: neighborhood_restaurant_information

CREATE TABLE restaurants (
  restaurant_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  neighbourhood TEXT ,
  href TEXT ,
  img TEXT ,
  star_rating REAL ,
  review_count INTEGER ,
  location TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  price_range INTEGER ,
  address TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  website TEXT ,
  operating_hours TEXT ,
  special_offers TEXT ,
  menu_link TEXT ,
  verified INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (restaurant_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (category) REFERENCES restaurant_categories (category_id)
);

CREATE TABLE reviews (
  review_id INTEGER ,
  restaurant_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  rating REAL ,
  review_text TEXT ,
  review_date TEXT ,
  verified INTEGER ,
  upvotes INTEGER ,
  downvotes INTEGER ,
  reported INTEGER ,
  response_text TEXT ,
  response_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (review_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (restaurant_id) REFERENCES restaurants (restaurant_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  profile_image TEXT ,
  bio TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE restaurant_categories (
  category_id INTEGER ,
  category_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (category_id)
);

-- Database: television_show_episode_management_751899

CREATE TABLE shows (
  show_id INTEGER ,
  show_title TEXT ,
  show_description TEXT ,
  network TEXT ,
  genre TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  runtime INTEGER ,
  country TEXT ,
  created_by TEXT ,
  production_company TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (show_id)
);

CREATE TABLE seasons (
  season_id INTEGER ,
  show_id INTEGER ,
  season_number INTEGER ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  total_episodes INTEGER ,
  budget REAL ,
  production_start_date TEXT ,
  production_end_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (season_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (show_id) REFERENCES shows (show_id)
);

CREATE TABLE episodes (
  episode_id INTEGER ,
  season_id INTEGER ,
  episode_num_in_season INTEGER ,
  episode_num_overall INTEGER ,
  title TEXT ,
  directed_by TEXT ,
  written_by TEXT ,
  original_air_date TEXT ,
  prod_code TEXT ,
  us_viewers REAL ,
  episode_type TEXT ,
  script_versions INTEGER ,
  production_notes TEXT ,
  viewership_trends TEXT ,
  international_audience TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (episode_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (season_id) REFERENCES seasons (season_id)
);

CREATE TABLE directors (
  director_id INTEGER ,
  director_name TEXT ,
  bio TEXT ,
  nationality TEXT ,
  awards TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (director_id)
);

CREATE TABLE writers (
  writer_id INTEGER ,
  writer_name TEXT ,
  bio TEXT ,
  nationality TEXT ,
  awards TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (writer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE episode_writers (
  episode_writer_id INTEGER ,
  episode_id INTEGER ,
  writer_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (episode_writer_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (episode_id) REFERENCES episodes (episode_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (writer_id) REFERENCES writers (writer_id)
);

CREATE TABLE episode_directors (
  episode_director_id INTEGER ,
  episode_id INTEGER ,
  director_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (episode_director_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (episode_id) REFERENCES episodes (episode_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (director_id) REFERENCES directors (director_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  access_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  episode_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  action TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (episode_id) REFERENCES episodes (episode_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE episode_ratings (
  rating_id INTEGER ,
  episode_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  rating REAL ,
  review TEXT ,
  review_date TEXT ,
  user_role TEXT ,
  thumbs_up_down TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rating_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (episode_id) REFERENCES episodes (episode_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: decentralized_finance__defi__liquidity_pool_management

CREATE TABLE liquidity_pools (
  pool_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  type TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  total_liquidity TEXT ,
  apy TEXT ,
  blockchain_network TEXT ,
  token_pair TEXT ,
  fee TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pool_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE gauges (
  gauge_id INTEGER ,
  pool_id INTEGER ,
  gauge_address TEXT ,
  gauge_type TEXT ,
  reward_token TEXT ,
  reward_amount TEXT ,
  start_time TEXT ,
  end_time TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (gauge_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pool_id) REFERENCES liquidity_pools (pool_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE bribe_addresses (
  bribe_id INTEGER ,
  pool_id INTEGER ,
  bribe_address TEXT ,
  bribe_type TEXT ,
  bribe_token TEXT ,
  bribe_amount TEXT ,
  start_time TEXT ,
  end_time TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (bribe_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pool_id) REFERENCES liquidity_pools (pool_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transactions (
  transaction_id INTEGER ,
  pool_id INTEGER ,
  transaction_hash TEXT ,
  block_number INTEGER ,
  transaction_type TEXT ,
  amount TEXT ,
  token TEXT ,
  from_address TEXT ,
  to_address TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  timestamp TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transaction_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pool_id) REFERENCES liquidity_pools (pool_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_pools (
  user_pool_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  pool_id INTEGER ,
  stake_amount TEXT ,
  stake_time TEXT ,
  unstake_time TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_pool_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pool_id) REFERENCES liquidity_pools (pool_id)
);

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  notification_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  message TEXT ,
  notification_type TEXT ,
  is_read INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  read_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (notification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE settings (
  setting_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  value TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (setting_id)
);

-- Database: nuclear_physics___radioactive_nuclides

CREATE TABLE nuclides (
  nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  nuclide_name TEXT ,
  isotopic_mass TEXT ,
  half_life TEXT ,
  mode_of_decay TEXT ,
  nuclear_spin TEXT ,
  magnetic_moment TEXT ,
  binding_energy TEXT ,
  natural_abundance TEXT ,
  nuclide_symbol TEXT ,
  atomic_number INTEGER ,
  neutron_number INTEGER ,
  isotopic_abundance TEXT ,
  discovery_year INTEGER ,
  discovery_team TEXT ,
  is_stable INTEGER ,
  decay_energy TEXT ,
  isotopic_composition TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (nuclide_id)
);

CREATE TABLE nuclide_decay_modes (
  decay_mode_id INTEGER ,
  decay_mode_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  parent_nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  decay_energy TEXT ,
  decay_constant TEXT ,
  half_life_uncertainty TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (decay_mode_id)
);

CREATE TABLE nuclide_decay_products (
  decay_product_id INTEGER ,
  nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  product_nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  branching_ratio TEXT ,
  decay_mode_id INTEGER ,
  product_isotopic_mass TEXT ,
  product_isotopic_abundance TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (decay_product_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (nuclide_id) REFERENCES nuclides (nuclide_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (product_nuclide_id) REFERENCES nuclides (nuclide_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (decay_mode_id) REFERENCES nuclide_decay_modes (decay_mode_id)
);

CREATE TABLE nuclide_measurements (
  measurement_id INTEGER ,
  nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  measurement_type TEXT ,
  value TEXT ,
  uncertainty TEXT ,
  measurement_date TEXT ,
  measurement_source TEXT ,
  measurement_method TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (measurement_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (nuclide_id) REFERENCES nuclides (nuclide_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  last_login_date TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  nuclide_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  access_duration INTEGER ,
  access_result TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (nuclide_id) REFERENCES nuclides (nuclide_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: pokemon_characteristics_and_effectiveness

CREATE TABLE pokemons (
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  pokedex_id INTEGER ,
  abilities TEXT ,
  typing TEXT ,
  hp INTEGER ,
  attack INTEGER ,
  defense INTEGER ,
  special_attack INTEGER ,
  special_defense INTEGER ,
  speed INTEGER ,
  height INTEGER ,
  weight INTEGER ,
  generation INTEGER ,
  female_rate REAL ,
  genderless INTEGER ,
  legendary_mythical INTEGER ,
  is_default INTEGER ,
  forms_switchable INTEGER ,
  base_experience INTEGER ,
  capture_rate INTEGER ,
  egg_groups TEXT ,
  base_happiness INTEGER ,
  primary_color TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_id)
);

CREATE TABLE types (
  type_id INTEGER ,
  type_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pokemon_types (
  pokemon_type_id INTEGER ,
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  type_id INTEGER ,
  typing_order INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemons (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (type_id) REFERENCES types (type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE effectiveness (
  effectiveness_id INTEGER ,
  type_id INTEGER ,
  normal_effectiveness REAL ,
  fire_effectiveness REAL ,
  water_effectiveness REAL ,
  electric_effectiveness REAL ,
  grass_effectiveness REAL ,
  ice_effectiveness REAL ,
  fighting_effectiveness REAL ,
  poison_effectiveness REAL ,
  ground_effectiveness REAL ,
  flying_effectiveness REAL ,
  psychic_effectiveness REAL ,
  bug_effectiveness REAL ,
  rock_effectiveness REAL ,
  ghost_effectiveness REAL ,
  dragon_effectiveness REAL ,
  dark_effectiveness REAL ,
  steel_effectiveness REAL ,
  fairy_effectiveness REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (effectiveness_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (type_id) REFERENCES types (type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE evolutions (
  evolution_id INTEGER ,
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  evolves_from TEXT ,
  evolves_into TEXT ,
  evolution_level INTEGER ,
  evolution_item TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (evolution_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemons (pokemon_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pokemon_effectiveness (
  pokemon_effectiveness_id INTEGER ,
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  effectiveness_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_effectiveness_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemons (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (effectiveness_id) REFERENCES effectiveness (effectiveness_id)
);

CREATE TABLE generations (
  generation_id INTEGER ,
  generation_name TEXT ,
  release_year INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (generation_id)
);

CREATE TABLE regions (
  region_id INTEGER ,
  region_name TEXT ,
  generation_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (region_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (generation_id) REFERENCES generations (generation_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pokemon_regions (
  pokemon_region_id INTEGER ,
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  region_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_region_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemons (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (region_id) REFERENCES regions (region_id)
);

CREATE TABLE abilities (
  ability_id INTEGER ,
  ability_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (ability_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pokemon_abilities (
  pokemon_ability_id INTEGER ,
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  ability_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_ability_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemons (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ability_id) REFERENCES abilities (ability_id)
);

-- Database: sediment_core_analysis

CREATE TABLE expeditions (
  expedition_id INTEGER ,
  expedition_name TEXT ,
  start_date TEXT ,
  end_date TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  project_lead TEXT ,
  research_vessel TEXT ,
  expedition_objective TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (expedition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE cores (
  core_id INTEGER ,
  expedition_id INTEGER ,
  core_name TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  latitude REAL ,
  longitude REAL ,
  core_length REAL ,
  core_diameter REAL ,
  core_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (core_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (expedition_id) REFERENCES expeditions (expedition_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sections (
  section_id INTEGER ,
  core_id INTEGER ,
  section_name TEXT ,
  top_interval_depth REAL ,
  bottom_interval_depth REAL ,
  section_length REAL ,
  section_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (section_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (core_id) REFERENCES cores (core_id)
);

CREATE TABLE subsection (
  subsection_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  subsection_name TEXT ,
  top_interval_depth REAL ,
  bottom_interval_depth REAL ,
  subsection_length REAL ,
  subsection_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (subsection_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lithology (
  lithology_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  lithology_description TEXT ,
  lithology_codes TEXT ,
  rock_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (lithology_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE colors (
  color_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  color_description TEXT ,
  color_codes TEXT ,
  color_intensity TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (color_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE structures (
  structure_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  structure_description TEXT ,
  structure_codes TEXT ,
  structure_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (structure_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE deformations (
  deformation_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  deformation_description TEXT ,
  deformation_codes TEXT ,
  deformationSeverity TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (deformation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE unusual_occurrences (
  occurrence_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  occurrence_description TEXT ,
  occurrence_codes TEXT ,
  occurrence_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (occurrence_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE minerals (
  mineral_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  mineral_description TEXT ,
  mineral_codes TEXT ,
  mineral_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (mineral_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE paleontology (
  paleontology_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  paleontology_description TEXT ,
  paleontology_codes TEXT ,
  paleontology_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (paleontology_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE other_observations (
  observation_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  observation_description TEXT ,
  observation_codes TEXT ,
  observation_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (observation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE hardness_induration (
  hardness_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  hardness_description TEXT ,
  hardness_codes TEXT ,
  hardness_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (hardness_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

CREATE TABLE images (
  image_id INTEGER ,
  section_id INTEGER ,
  image_path TEXT ,
  image_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (image_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (section_id) REFERENCES sections (section_id)
);

-- Database: childrens_growth_measurements

CREATE TABLE growth_data (
  measurement_id INTEGER ,
  child_id INTEGER ,
  age_group_id INTEGER ,
  height REAL ,
  chest REAL ,
  waist REAL ,
  hip REAL ,
  weight REAL ,
  head_circumference REAL ,
  BMI REAL ,
  z_score_height REAL ,
  z_score_weight REAL ,
  z_score_BMI REAL ,
  percentile_height REAL ,
  percentile_weight REAL ,
  percentile_BMI REAL ,
  measurement_date TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (measurement_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (child_id) REFERENCES children (child_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (age_group_id) REFERENCES age_groups (age_group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE children (
  child_id INTEGER ,
  child_name TEXT ,
  date_of_birth TEXT ,
  gender TEXT ,
  parent_id INTEGER ,
  medical_conditions TEXT ,
  allergies TEXT ,
  last_visit_date TEXT ,
  next_visit_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (child_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES parents (parent_id)
);

CREATE TABLE age_groups (
  age_group_id INTEGER ,
  age_range TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (age_group_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  organization TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  parent_child_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_child_id) REFERENCES children (child_id)
);

CREATE TABLE parents (
  parent_id INTEGER ,
  parent_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (parent_id)
);

CREATE TABLE communications (
  communication_id INTEGER ,
  child_id INTEGER ,
  parent_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  communication_date TEXT ,
  communication_type TEXT ,
  message TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (communication_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (child_id) REFERENCES children (child_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES parents (parent_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE educational_content (
  content_id INTEGER ,
  title TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  content_type TEXT ,
  url TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (content_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_access_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  activity TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

-- Database: blockchain_and_decentralized_finance__defi__governance

CREATE TABLE members (
  member_id INTEGER ,
  member_address TEXT ,
  moloch_address TEXT ,
  network TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  shares INTEGER ,
  loot INTEGER ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  roles TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  last_activity TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (member_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (moloch_address) REFERENCES moloch_contracts (moloch_address), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES members (member_id)
);

CREATE TABLE moloch_contracts (
  contract_id INTEGER ,
  moloch_address TEXT ,
  network TEXT ,
  deployed_at TEXT ,
  total_shares INTEGER ,
  total_loot INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  total_members INTEGER ,
  total_transactions INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (contract_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES members (member_id)
);

CREATE TABLE proposals (
  proposal_id INTEGER ,
  moloch_address TEXT ,
  network TEXT ,
  proposer_id INTEGER ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  vote_start TEXT ,
  vote_end TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  total_votes INTEGER ,
  yes_votes INTEGER ,
  no_votes INTEGER ,
  abstain_votes INTEGER ,
  voting_power REAL ,
  created_by INTEGER ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (proposal_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (proposer_id) REFERENCES members (member_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES members (member_id)
);

CREATE TABLE votes (
  vote_id INTEGER ,
  proposal_id INTEGER ,
  member_id INTEGER ,
  voted_at TEXT ,
  vote_type TEXT ,
  voting_power REAL ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  is_weighted INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (vote_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (proposal_id) REFERENCES proposals (proposal_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (member_id) REFERENCES members (member_id)
);

CREATE TABLE transactions (
  transaction_id INTEGER ,
  member_id INTEGER ,
  proposal_id INTEGER ,
  moloch_address TEXT ,
  network TEXT ,
  transaction_type TEXT ,
  amount REAL ,
  transaction_time TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  fee REAL ,
  hash TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  is_reverted INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (transaction_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (member_id) REFERENCES members (member_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (proposal_id) REFERENCES proposals (proposal_id)
);

-- Database: indian_state_information_and_governance

CREATE TABLE states (
  state_id INTEGER ,
  state_name TEXT ,
  capital TEXT ,
  population TEXT ,
  area_sq_km REAL ,
  official_language TEXT ,
  founding_date TEXT ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  geographic_region TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (state_id)
);

CREATE TABLE state_birds (
  bird_id INTEGER ,
  state_id INTEGER ,
  bird_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (bird_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (state_id) REFERENCES states (state_id)
);

CREATE TABLE state_animals (
  animal_id INTEGER ,
  state_id INTEGER ,
  animal_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (animal_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (state_id) REFERENCES states (state_id)
);

-- Database: exoplanet_discovery_and_characterization

CREATE TABLE exoplanets (
  exoplanet_id INTEGER ,
  planet_name TEXT ,
  host_name TEXT ,
  number_of_stars INTEGER ,
  detection_method TEXT ,
  orbital_period REAL ,
  orbit_semi_major_axis REAL ,
  planet_radius REAL ,
  planet_mass REAL ,
  insolation_flux REAL ,
  equilibrium_temperature REAL ,
  habitable_zone_flag INTEGER ,
  planet_classification TEXT ,
  planet_type TEXT ,
  discovery_date TEXT ,
  discovery_team TEXT ,
  atmospheric_composition TEXT ,
  magnetic_field_strength REAL ,
  albedo REAL ,
  eccentricity REAL ,
  inclination REAL ,
  surface_gravity REAL ,
  age TEXT ,
  distance_from_host_star REAL ,
  star_id INTEGER ,
  detection_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (exoplanet_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (host_name) REFERENCES stars (host_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (detection_method) REFERENCES detections (detection_method), 
  FOREIGN KEY (star_id) REFERENCES stars (star_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (detection_id) REFERENCES detections (detection_id)
);

CREATE TABLE stars (
  star_id INTEGER ,
  host_name TEXT ,
  stellar_effective_temperature REAL ,
  stellar_radius REAL ,
  stellar_mass REAL ,
  ra_degree REAL ,
  dec_degree REAL ,
  distance REAL ,
  stellar_luminosity REAL ,
  stellar_spectral_type TEXT ,
  stellar_age TEXT ,
  metallicity REAL ,
  stellar_magnetic_field_strength REAL ,
  stellar_rotation_period REAL ,
  stellar_activity_level TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (star_id)
);

CREATE TABLE detections (
  detection_id INTEGER ,
  detection_method TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  first_used TEXT ,
  last_used TEXT ,
  accuracy REAL ,
  sensitivity REAL ,
  cost REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (detection_id)
);

-- Database: gpu_kernel_execution_and_profiling

CREATE TABLE devices (
  device_id INTEGER ,
  device_name TEXT ,
  device_type TEXT ,
  memory_capacity INTEGER ,
  compute_capability TEXT ,
  multi_processor_count INTEGER ,
  max_threads_per_block INTEGER ,
  max_shared_memory_per_block INTEGER ,
  max_grid_size INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (device_id)
);

CREATE TABLE kernels (
  kernel_id INTEGER ,
  kernel_name TEXT ,
  kernel_type TEXT ,
  device_id INTEGER ,
  num_blocks INTEGER ,
  num_threads_per_block INTEGER ,
  shared_memory_size INTEGER ,
  local_memory_size INTEGER ,
  register_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (kernel_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (device_id) REFERENCES devices (device_id)
);

CREATE TABLE kernel_launches (
  launch_id INTEGER ,
  kernel_id INTEGER ,
  launch_type TEXT ,
  grid_dim_x INTEGER ,
  grid_dim_y INTEGER ,
  grid_dim_z INTEGER ,
  block_dim_x INTEGER ,
  block_dim_y INTEGER ,
  block_dim_z INTEGER ,
  dynamic_shared_memory_size INTEGER ,
  stream_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (launch_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (kernel_id) REFERENCES kernels (kernel_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (launch_type) REFERENCES launch_types (launch_type_id)
);

CREATE TABLE execution_profiles (
  profile_id INTEGER ,
  launch_id INTEGER ,
  start_time INTEGER ,
  end_time INTEGER ,
  execution_time INTEGER ,
  memory_usage INTEGER ,
  occupancy REAL ,
  launch_latency INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (profile_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (launch_id) REFERENCES kernel_launches (launch_id)
);

CREATE TABLE memory_configs (
  config_id INTEGER ,
  launch_id INTEGER ,
  shared_memory INTEGER ,
  local_memory_per_thread INTEGER ,
  local_memory_total INTEGER ,
  constants_memory INTEGER ,
  texture_memory INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (config_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (launch_id) REFERENCES kernel_launches (launch_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_time INTEGER ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  access_ip TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE kernel_names (
  name_id INTEGER ,
  kernel_name TEXT ,
  long_kernel_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (name_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (kernel_name) REFERENCES kernels (kernel_name)
);

CREATE TABLE contexts (
  context_id INTEGER ,
  context_name TEXT ,
  device_id INTEGER ,
  context_flags INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (context_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (device_id) REFERENCES devices (device_id)
);

CREATE TABLE streams (
  stream_id INTEGER ,
  stream_name TEXT ,

-- Database: air_travel_and_flight_information_management

CREATE TABLE flights (
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  year INTEGER ,
  month INTEGER ,
  day INTEGER ,
  dep_time INTEGER ,
  sched_dep_time INTEGER ,
  dep_delay INTEGER ,
  arr_time INTEGER ,
  sched_arr_time INTEGER ,
  arr_delay INTEGER ,
  carrier TEXT ,
  flight_num INTEGER ,
  tailnum TEXT ,
  origin TEXT ,
  dest TEXT ,
  flight_type TEXT ,
  flight_distance INTEGER ,
  aircraft_model TEXT ,
  capacity INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (flight_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (carrier) REFERENCES carriers (code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (tailnum) REFERENCES planes (tailnum), 
  FOREIGN KEY (origin) REFERENCES airports (code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dest) REFERENCES airports (code)
);

CREATE TABLE airports (
  airport_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  code TEXT ,
  city TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  latitude TEXT ,
  longitude TEXT ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  elevation INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (airport_id)
);

CREATE TABLE carriers (
  carrier_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  code TEXT ,
  headquarters TEXT ,
  website TEXT ,
  founding_year INTEGER ,
  fleet_size INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (carrier_id)
);

CREATE TABLE planes (
  plane_id INTEGER ,
  tailnum TEXT ,
  type TEXT ,
  manufacturer TEXT ,
  year_of_manufacture INTEGER ,
  seats INTEGER ,
  range INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (plane_id)
);

CREATE TABLE passengers (
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  passport_number TEXT ,
  travel_class TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE bookings (
  booking_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  booking_date TEXT ,
  seat_number TEXT ,
  price TEXT ,
  payment_method TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (booking_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (passenger_id) REFERENCES passengers (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE check_ins (
  check_in_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  check_in_date TEXT ,
  gate_number TEXT ,
  baggage_count INTEGER ,
  seat_assignment TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (check_in_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (passenger_id) REFERENCES passengers (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE baggage_claims (
  baggage_claim_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  baggage_claim_date TEXT ,
  claim_number TEXT ,
  reason TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (baggage_claim_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (passenger_id) REFERENCES passengers (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_delays (
  delay_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  delay_reason TEXT ,
  delay_duration INTEGER ,
  estimated_arrival TEXT ,
  compensation TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (delay_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_cancellations (
  cancellation_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  cancellation_reason TEXT ,
  cancellation_date TEXT ,
  rescheduled_flight_id INTEGER ,
  compensation TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (cancellation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (rescheduled_flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE passenger_notifications (
  notification_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  notification_date TEXT ,
  notification_type TEXT ,
  message TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (notification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (passenger_id) REFERENCES passengers (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_reroutings (
  rerouting_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  rerouting_reason TEXT ,
  new_route TEXT ,
  estimated_arrival TEXT ,
  new_aircraft_model TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rerouting_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_diversions (
  diversion_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  diversion_reason TEXT ,
  new_destination TEXT ,
  estimated_arrival TEXT ,
  new_aircraft_model TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (diversion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_status_updates (
  update_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  update_date TEXT ,
  gate_number TEXT ,
  terminal TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (update_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

-- Database: algorithmic_problem_solving_platform

CREATE TABLE problems (
  problem_id INTEGER ,

  acceptance_rate REAL ,
  difficulty TEXT ,
  frequency REAL ,
  link TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  author_id INTEGER ,
  views_count INTEGER ,
  likes_count INTEGER ,
  dislikes_count INTEGER ,
  upvotes_count INTEGER ,
  downvotes_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (problem_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (author_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  tag_id INTEGER ,

  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  views_count INTEGER ,
  likes_count INTEGER ,
  dislikes_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (tag_id),

CREATE TABLE forum_topics (
  topic_id INTEGER ,

  topic_name TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  views_count INTEGER ,
  likes_count INTEGER ,
  dislikes_count INTEGER ,
  upvotes_count INTEGER ,
  downvotes_count INTEGER ,

CREATE TABLE forum_posts (
  post_id INTEGER ,
  topic_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  post_date TEXT ,
  post_content TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  views_count INTEGER ,
  likes_count INTEGER ,
  dislikes_count INTEGER ,
  upvotes_count INTEGER ,
  downvotes_count INTEGER ,
  edited_by INTEGER ,
  edited_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (post_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (topic_id) REFERENCES forum_topics (topic_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (edited_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE comments (
  comment_id INTEGER ,
  post_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  comment_date TEXT ,
  comment_content TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  views_count INTEGER ,
  likes_count INTEGER ,
  dislikes_count INTEGER ,
  upvotes_count INTEGER ,
  downvotes_count INTEGER ,
  edited_by INTEGER ,
  edited_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (comment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (post_id) REFERENCES forum_posts (post_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (edited_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE leaderboard (

-- Database: star_wars_galactic_missions_and_rewards

CREATE TABLE missions (
  mission_id INTEGER ,
  mission_name TEXT ,
  faction_id INTEGER ,
  type TEXT ,
  reward_id INTEGER ,
  reputation_id INTEGER ,
  how_to_get TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  difficulty TEXT ,

  required_level INTEGER ,
  start_npc INTEGER ,
  end_npc INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (mission_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (faction_id) REFERENCES factions (faction_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (reward_id) REFERENCES rewards (reward_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (reputation_id) REFERENCES reputations (reputation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (location) REFERENCES locations (location_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (start_npc) REFERENCES npcs (npc_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (end_npc) REFERENCES npcs (npc_id)
);

  mass_error_ppm REAL ,

  PRIMARY KEY (spec_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (researcher_id) REFERENCES researchers (researcher_id)
);

CREATE TABLE peptides (
  peptide_id INTEGER ,
  sequence TEXT ,
  protein TEXT ,
  denovo_score REAL ,
  msgf_score REAL ,
  spec_prob REAL ,
  p_value REAL ,
  fdr REAL ,
  pepfdr REAL ,
  modifications TEXT ,
  peptide_length INTEGER ,
  source_organism TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (peptide_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (protein) REFERENCES proteins (accession)
);

CREATE TABLE proteins (
  protein_id INTEGER ,
  accession TEXT ,
  gene_symbol TEXT ,
  tax_id INTEGER ,
  protein_name TEXT ,
  gene_description TEXT ,
  protein_length INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (protein_id)
);

CREATE TABLE spectra_peptides (
  spec_id INTEGER ,
  peptide_id INTEGER ,
  confidence_score REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (spec_id, peptide_id)
);

CREATE TABLE researchers (
  researcher_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  institution TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (researcher_id)
);

CREATE TABLE experiments (
  experiment_id INTEGER ,
  experiment_name TEXT ,
  date_conducted TEXT ,
  researcher_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (experiment_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (researcher_id) REFERENCES researchers (researcher_id)
);

-- Database: online_auction_bidding

CREATE TABLE auctions (
  auction_id INTEGER ,
  item_id INTEGER ,
  start_time TEXT ,
  end_time TEXT ,
  reserve_price INTEGER ,
  minimum_bid INTEGER ,
  current_highest_bid INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  seller_id INTEGER ,
  auction_type TEXT ,
  bid_increment INTEGER ,
  status_updated_at TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  rules TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (auction_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (item_id) REFERENCES items (item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (seller_id) REFERENCES sellers (seller_id)
);

CREATE TABLE bids (
  bid_id INTEGER ,
  bid_time TEXT ,
  bid_amount INTEGER ,
  bidder_id INTEGER ,
  auction_id INTEGER ,
  bid_status TEXT ,
  bid_withdrawn_at TEXT ,
  bid_withdrawn_by INTEGER ,
  bid_source TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (bid_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (bidder_id) REFERENCES bidders (bidder_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (auction_id) REFERENCES auctions (auction_id)
);

CREATE TABLE bidders (
  bidder_id INTEGER ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  bid_count INTEGER ,
  total_spent INTEGER ,
  date_joined TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  verified INTEGER ,
  referral_code TEXT ,
  credit_score INTEGER ,
  is_suspended INTEGER ,
  suspension_reason TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (bidder_id)
);

CREATE TABLE items (
  item_id INTEGER ,
  item_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  starting_bid INTEGER ,
  image_url TEXT ,
  condition TEXT ,
  weight REAL ,
  dimensions TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  shipping_info TEXT ,
  seller_id INTEGER ,
  view_count INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (item_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (seller_id) REFERENCES sellers (seller_id)
);

CREATE TABLE sellers (
  seller_id INTEGER ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  date_joined TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  verified INTEGER ,
  credit_score INTEGER ,
  is_suspended INTEGER ,
  suspension_reason TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (seller_id)
);

CREATE TABLE auction_rules (
  rule_id INTEGER ,
  auction_id INTEGER ,
  rule_type TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (rule_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (auction_id) REFERENCES auctions (auction_id)
);

CREATE TABLE bid_history (
  history_id INTEGER ,
  bid_id INTEGER ,
  action TEXT ,
  action_time TEXT ,
  action_by INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (history_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (bid_id) REFERENCES bids (bid_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (action_by) REFERENCES bidders (bidder_id)
);

-- Database: aviation_flight_log_and_routing_management

CREATE TABLE flights (
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  leg_number TEXT ,
  flight_number TEXT ,
  aircraft_id TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  airport_departure TEXT ,
  date_departure TEXT ,
  hour_departure TEXT ,
  actual_departure_time TEXT ,
  airport_arrival TEXT ,
  date_arrival TEXT ,
  hour_arrival TEXT ,
  actual_arrival_time TEXT ,
  gate_departure TEXT ,
  gate_arrival TEXT ,
  route_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (flight_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (airport_departure) REFERENCES airports (airport_code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (airport_arrival) REFERENCES airports (airport_code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (route_id) REFERENCES routes (route_id)
);

CREATE TABLE airports (
  airport_code TEXT ,
  airport_name TEXT ,
  location TEXT ,
  latitude REAL ,
  longitude REAL ,
  elevation INTEGER ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  contact_number TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (airport_code)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  first_name TEXT ,
  last_name TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_logs (
  log_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  event_type TEXT ,
  event_description TEXT ,
  event_time TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  status_before TEXT ,
  status_after TEXT ,
  remarks TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (log_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE routes (
  route_id INTEGER ,
  departure_airport TEXT ,
  arrival_airport TEXT ,
  duration INTEGER ,
  distance INTEGER ,
  frequency TEXT ,
  popularity INTEGER ,
  operational_efficiency REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (route_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (departure_airport) REFERENCES airports (airport_code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (arrival_airport) REFERENCES airports (airport_code)
);

-- Database: genomic_peak_annotations_and_gene_information

CREATE TABLE genomic_peaks (
  peak_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  chromosome TEXT ,
  start_position INTEGER ,
  end_position INTEGER ,
  signal_strength REAL ,
  q_value REAL ,
  peak_index INTEGER ,
  width INTEGER ,
  source_dataset_id INTEGER ,
  peak_type TEXT ,
  peak_quality_score REAL ,
  annotated_by INTEGER ,
  annotation_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (peak_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (chromosome) REFERENCES chromosomes (chromosome_name), 
  FOREIGN KEY (source_dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (annotated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE genes (
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  ensembl_id TEXT ,
  entrez_id REAL ,
  external_name TEXT ,
  chromosome TEXT ,
  start_position INTEGER ,
  end_position INTEGER ,
  strand TEXT ,
  gene_biotype TEXT ,
  gene_description TEXT ,
  gene_expression_levels TEXT ,
  gene_function TEXT ,
  curated_by INTEGER ,
  curation_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (curated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE chromosomes (
  chromosome_id INTEGER ,
  chromosome_name TEXT ,
  length INTEGER ,
  assembly_version TEXT ,
  species TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (chromosome_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  password_hash TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  team_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE datasets (
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  upload_date TEXT ,
  source TEXT ,
  version TEXT ,
  species TEXT ,
  curated_by INTEGER ,
  curation_date TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (curated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dataset_peaks (
  dataset_peak_id INTEGER ,
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  peak_id INTEGER ,
  peak_confidence REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dataset_peak_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (peak_id) REFERENCES genomic_peaks (peak_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  dataset_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  user_agent TEXT ,
  access_duration INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dataset_id) REFERENCES datasets (dataset_id)
);

CREATE TABLE train_schedules (
  schedule_id INTEGER ,
  train_id INTEGER ,
  station_id INTEGER ,
  scheduled_arrival TEXT ,
  actual_arrival TEXT ,
  scheduled_departure TEXT ,
  actual_departure TEXT ,
  halt_time INTEGER ,
  train_status TEXT ,
  day_count INTEGER ,
  platform_number TEXT ,
  train_status_reason TEXT ,
  delay_minutes INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  notes TEXT ,
  next_station_id INTEGER ,
  next_station_estimated_arrival TEXT ,
  next_station_estimated_departure TEXT ,
  is_cancelled INTEGER ,
  cancellation_reason TEXT ,

  FOREIGN KEY (train_id) REFERENCES trains (train_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (station_id) REFERENCES stations (station_id)
);

CREATE TABLE trains (
  train_id INTEGER ,

  operating_days TEXT ,

  last_maintenance_date TEXT ,

);

CREATE TABLE routes (
  route_id INTEGER ,

  start_station_id INTEGER ,
  end_station_id INTEGER ,
  total_stations INTEGER ,
  route_description TEXT ,
  total_distance TEXT ,
  average_travel_time TEXT ,
  route_frequency TEXT ,
  route_capacity INTEGER ,

  FOREIGN KEY (end_station_id) REFERENCES stations (station_id)

-- Database: food_based_correlation_analysis_for_enzyme_discovery

CREATE TABLE foods (

  PRIMARY KEY (food_id)

  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (enzyme_id)

  hold_date TEXT ,
  expiry_date TEXT ,
  notification_sent TEXT ,
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  pickup_date TEXT ,
  pickup_location TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (hold_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (desk_id) REFERENCES circulation_desks (desk_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (patron_id) REFERENCES patrons (patron_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (material_id) REFERENCES materials (material_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES library_branches (branch_id)
);

CREATE TABLE patrons (
  patron_id INTEGER ,
  patron_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  library_card_number TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  registration_date TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (patron_id)
);

CREATE TABLE materials (
  material_id INTEGER ,
  material_title TEXT ,
  author TEXT ,
  publisher TEXT ,
  publish_date TEXT ,
  isbn TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  edition TEXT ,
  language TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (material_id)
);

CREATE TABLE checkouts (
  checkout_id INTEGER ,
  patron_id INTEGER ,
  material_id INTEGER ,
  checkout_date TEXT ,
  due_date TEXT ,
  return_date TEXT ,
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  checkout_location TEXT ,
  renewal_count INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (checkout_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (patron_id) REFERENCES patrons (patron_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (material_id) REFERENCES materials (material_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES library_branches (branch_id)
);

CREATE TABLE returns (
  return_id INTEGER ,
  patron_id INTEGER ,
  material_id INTEGER ,
  return_date TEXT ,
  desk_id INTEGER ,
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  return_location TEXT ,
  condition TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (return_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (patron_id) REFERENCES patrons (patron_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (material_id) REFERENCES materials (material_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (desk_id) REFERENCES circulation_desks (desk_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES library_branches (branch_id)
);

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  notification_id INTEGER ,
  patron_id INTEGER ,
  material_id INTEGER ,
  notification_date TEXT ,
  notification_type TEXT ,
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  sent_by TEXT ,
  message TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (notification_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (patron_id) REFERENCES patrons (patron_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (material_id) REFERENCES materials (material_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES library_branches (branch_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sent_by) REFERENCES staff (staff_id)
);

CREATE TABLE library_branches (
  branch_id INTEGER ,
  branch_name TEXT ,
  address TEXT ,
  phone_number TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  created_date TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (branch_id)
);

-- Database: titanic_passenger_data_analysis_537022

CREATE TABLE passengers (
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  survived INTEGER ,
  pclass INTEGER ,
  sibsp INTEGER ,
  parch INTEGER ,
  fare REAL ,
  sex_female INTEGER ,
  sex_male INTEGER ,
  embarked_c INTEGER ,
  embarked_q INTEGER ,
  embarked_s INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  age INTEGER ,
  ticket_number TEXT ,
  cabin_number TEXT ,
  passport_number TEXT ,
  occupation TEXT ,
  nationality TEXT ,
  marital_status TEXT ,
  title TEXT ,
  relatives_onboard TEXT ,
  travel_reason TEXT ,
  survivor_location TEXT ,
  body_recovered INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (passenger_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sex_female) REFERENCES gender (gender_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (sex_male) REFERENCES gender (gender_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (embarked_c) REFERENCES embarkation_ports (port_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (embarked_q) REFERENCES embarkation_ports (port_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (embarked_s) REFERENCES embarkation_ports (port_id)
);

CREATE TABLE gender (
  gender_id INTEGER ,
  gender_name TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (gender_id)
);

CREATE TABLE embarkation_ports (
  port_id INTEGER ,
  port_name TEXT ,
  port_code TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (port_id)
);

CREATE TABLE passenger_analysis (
  analysis_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  survival_rate REAL ,
  average_fare REAL ,
  average_sibsp REAL ,
  average_parch REAL ,
  average_age REAL ,
  average_occupation TEXT ,
  average_nationality TEXT ,
  average_marital_status TEXT ,
  average_travel_reason TEXT ,
  survival_rate_by_age REAL ,
  survival_rate_by_occupation REAL ,
  survival_rate_by_nationality REAL ,
  survival_rate_by_marital_status REAL ,
  survival_rate_by_travel_reason REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (analysis_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (passenger_id) REFERENCES passengers (passenger_id)
);

-- Database: mmorpg__massively_multiplayer_online_role_playing_game__skill_and_buff_stat

CREATE TABLE skills (
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  skill_name TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  cooldown_time INTEGER ,
  mana_cost INTEGER ,
  is_passive INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE buffs (
  buff_id INTEGER ,
  buff_name TEXT ,
  category TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  duration INTEGER ,
  stacks INTEGER ,
  is_renewable INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (buff_id)
);

CREATE TABLE skill_statistics (
  stat_id INTEGER ,
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  average_effectiveness REAL ,
  min_effectiveness REAL ,
  max_effectiveness REAL ,
  average_duration REAL ,
  min_duration REAL ,
  max_duration REAL ,
  average_cooldown_time REAL ,
  min_cooldown_time REAL ,
  max_cooldown_time REAL ,
  average_mana_cost REAL ,
  min_mana_cost REAL ,
  max_mana_cost REAL ,
  PRIMARY KEY (stat_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE buff_statistics (
  stat_id INTEGER ,
  buff_id INTEGER ,
  average_effectiveness REAL ,
  min_effectiveness REAL ,
  max_effectiveness REAL ,
  average_duration REAL ,
  min_duration REAL ,
  max_duration REAL ,
  average_stacks REAL ,
  min_stacks INTEGER ,
  max_stacks INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (stat_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id)

  FOREIGN KEY (skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dependent_skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE skill_synergies (
  synergy_id INTEGER ,
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  synergistic_skill_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (synergy_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (synergistic_skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE skill_exclusions (
  exclusion_id INTEGER ,
  skill_id INTEGER ,
  excluded_skill_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (exclusion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (excluded_skill_id) REFERENCES skills (skill_id)
);

CREATE TABLE buff_dependencies (
  dependency_id INTEGER ,
  buff_id INTEGER ,
  dependent_buff_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (dependency_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (dependent_buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id)
);

CREATE TABLE buff_synergies (
  synergy_id INTEGER ,

  FOREIGN KEY (buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (synergistic_buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id)

CREATE TABLE buff_exclusions (

  PRIMARY KEY (exclusion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (excluded_buff_id) REFERENCES buffs (buff_id)
);

-- Database: pokemon_species_attributes_and_abilities

CREATE TABLE pokemon_species (
  pokemon_id INTEGER ,
  name TEXT ,
  evolution_stage INTEGER ,
  normal_type TEXT ,
  secondary_type TEXT ,
  height REAL ,
  weight REAL ,
  ability TEXT ,
  base_hp INTEGER ,
  base_attack INTEGER ,
  base_defense INTEGER ,
  base_speed INTEGER ,
  base_special_attack INTEGER ,
  base_special_defense INTEGER ,
  generation_id INTEGER ,
  color TEXT ,
  shape_id INTEGER ,
  habitat_id INTEGER ,
  is_legendary INTEGER ,
  is_mythical INTEGER ,
  is_ultra_beast INTEGER ,
  gender_ratio TEXT ,
  capture_rate INTEGER ,
  base_experience INTEGER ,
  growth_rate_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (evolution_stage) REFERENCES evolution_stages (evolution_stage_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (normal_type) REFERENCES types (type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (secondary_type) REFERENCES types (type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ability) REFERENCES abilities (ability_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (generation_id) REFERENCES generations (generation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (shape_id) REFERENCES shapes (shape_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (habitat_id) REFERENCES habitats (habitat_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (growth_rate_id) REFERENCES growth_rates (growth_rate_id)
);

  evolution_stage_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (evolution_stage_id)
);

  description TEXT ,
  effectiveness_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (type_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (effectiveness_id) REFERENCES type_effectiveness (type_id)

CREATE TABLE abilities (
  ability_id INTEGER ,

  effect TEXT ,
  hidden_ability INTEGER ,
  flavor_text TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (ability_id)
);

  iv_special_defense INTEGER ,
  ev_hp INTEGER ,
  ev_attack INTEGER ,
  ev_defense INTEGER ,
  ev_speed INTEGER ,
  ev_special_attack INTEGER ,
  ev_special_defense INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (owner_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (pokemon_id) REFERENCES pokemon_species (pokemon_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES players (player_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (nature_id) REFERENCES natures (nature_id)
);

-- Database: sql_function_and_aggregation_operations

CREATE TABLE functions (
  function_id INTEGER ,
  function_name TEXT ,
  argument_types TEXT ,
  return_type TEXT ,
  partial_mode TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,

  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_by INTEGER ,
  updated_at TEXT ,

  error_handling TEXT ,
  data_validation TEXT ,

  PRIMARY KEY (function_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (updated_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  last_login TEXT ,
  is_admin INTEGER ,
  is_active INTEGER ,
  last_password_change TEXT ,
  failed_login_attempts INTEGER ,
  lockout_time TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

  usage_id INTEGER ,
  function_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  usage_date TEXT ,

  result TEXT ,

  is_successful INTEGER ,
  error_message TEXT ,

  memory_usage REAL ,
  cpu_time REAL ,
  input_data TEXT ,
  output_data TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (usage_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (function_id) REFERENCES functions (function_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  log_id INTEGER ,
  function_id INTEGER ,

  output_data TEXT ,

  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users (user_id)

  FOREIGN KEY (crew_id) REFERENCES crews (crew_id)

  esxi_phys_host_id TEXT ,

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  entity_id TEXT ,
  entity_type TEXT ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  user_role TEXT ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  access_ip TEXT ,
  access_location TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id),

);

-- Database: cryptographic_protocols_and_cipher_suites

CREATE TABLE cipher_suites (
  suite_id INTEGER ,
  cipher_suite_name TEXT ,
  protocol_version TEXT ,
  encryption_algorithm_id INTEGER ,
  digest_algorithm_id INTEGER ,
  key_establishment_id INTEGER ,
  auth_method_id INTEGER ,
  key_size INTEGER ,
  is_deprecated INTEGER ,
  security_rating INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (suite_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (encryption_algorithm_id) REFERENCES encryption_algorithms (algorithm_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (digest_algorithm_id) REFERENCES digest_algorithms (digest_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (key_establishment_id) REFERENCES key_establishment_methods (method_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (auth_method_id) REFERENCES auth_methods (method_id)
);

CREATE TABLE encryption_algorithms (
  algorithm_id INTEGER ,
  algorithm_name TEXT ,
  block_size INTEGER ,
  mode_of_operation TEXT ,
  key_size_range TEXT ,
  is_stream_cipher INTEGER ,
  is_block_cipher INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (algorithm_id)
);

CREATE TABLE digest_algorithms (
  digest_id INTEGER ,
  digest_name TEXT ,
  output_size INTEGER ,
  is_hash_function INTEGER ,
  is_mac_algorithm INTEGER ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (digest_id)
);

CREATE TABLE cipher_suite_details (
  detail_id INTEGER ,
  suite_id INTEGER ,
  encryption_algorithm_id INTEGER ,
  digest_algorithm_id INTEGER ,
  key_establishment_id INTEGER ,
  auth_method_id INTEGER ,
  is_secure INTEGER ,
  security_notes TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (detail_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (suite_id) REFERENCES cipher_suites (suite_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (encryption_algorithm_id) REFERENCES encryption_algorithms (algorithm_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (digest_algorithm_id) REFERENCES digest_algorithms (digest_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (key_establishment_id) REFERENCES key_establishment_methods (method_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (auth_method_id) REFERENCES auth_methods (method_id)
);

CREATE TABLE key_establishment_methods (
  method_id INTEGER ,
  method_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (method_id)
);

CREATE TABLE auth_methods (
  method_id INTEGER ,
  method_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  last_updated TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (method_id)
);

-- Database: genetic_disease_association_and_dosage_sensitivity

CREATE TABLE genes (
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  gene_symbol TEXT ,

  chromosome TEXT ,

  end_position INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (gene_id)
);

  disease_id INTEGER ,
  disease_label TEXT ,
  mondo_id TEXT ,
  disease_url TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  disease_type TEXT ,
  age_of_onset TEXT ,
  prevalence TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (disease_id)
);

CREATE TABLE gene_disease_associations (
  association_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  disease_id INTEGER ,
  mode_of_inheritance TEXT ,

  association_type TEXT ,
  confidence_level TEXT ,
  citation TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (association_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disease_id) REFERENCES diseases (disease_id)
);

  assertion_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  disease_id INTEGER ,

  evidence_level TEXT ,

  assertion_report TEXT ,
  dosage_type TEXT ,
  dosage_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (assertion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disease_id) REFERENCES diseases (disease_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dosage_triplosensitivity (
  assertion_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  disease_id INTEGER ,
  assertion_date TEXT ,
  evidence_level TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  assertion_report TEXT ,
  dosage_type TEXT ,
  dosage_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (assertion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disease_id) REFERENCES diseases (disease_id)
);

CREATE TABLE gene_disease_validity (
  assertion_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,

  description TEXT ,
  assertion_report TEXT ,
  validity_type TEXT ,
  validity_confidence TEXT ,
  evidence_code TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (assertion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disease_id) REFERENCES diseases (disease_id)
);

CREATE TABLE actionability (
  assertion_id INTEGER ,
  gene_id INTEGER ,
  disease_id INTEGER ,
  assertion_date TEXT ,
  classification TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  assertion_report TEXT ,
  actionability_type TEXT ,
  actionability_confidence TEXT ,
  clinical_significance TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (assertion_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (gene_id) REFERENCES genes (gene_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (disease_id) REFERENCES diseases (disease_id)
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  user_name TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  role TEXT ,
  institution TEXT ,
  department TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE access_logs (
  access_id INTEGER ,
  association_id INTEGER ,
  user_id INTEGER ,
  access_date TEXT ,
  access_type TEXT ,
  ip_address TEXT ,
  access_method TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (access_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (association_id) REFERENCES gene_disease_associations (association_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)

CREATE TABLE annotations (
  annotation_id INTEGER ,

  PRIMARY KEY (annotation_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (association_id) REFERENCES gene_disease_associations (association_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)

-- Database: magic_spell_management_and_rpg_mechanics

CREATE TABLE spells (
  spell_id INTEGER ,
  spell_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  base_damage INTEGER ,
  mana_cost INTEGER ,
  area_of_effect TEXT ,
  duration TEXT ,
  element_type TEXT ,
  target_type TEXT ,
  level_required INTEGER ,
  skill_points_required INTEGER ,
  is_learnable INTEGER ,
  icon TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (spell_id)
);

CREATE TABLE spell_mastery (
  mastery_id INTEGER ,
  spell_id INTEGER ,
  level INTEGER ,
  additional_damage INTEGER ,
  xp_bonus INTEGER ,
  heal_points INTEGER ,
  mana_cost_reduction INTEGER ,
  cooldown_reduction INTEGER ,
  effect_multipliers TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (mastery_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (spell_id) REFERENCES spells (spell_id)
);

CREATE TABLE players (
  player_id INTEGER ,
  player_name TEXT ,
  class TEXT ,
  level INTEGER ,
  mana INTEGER ,
  gold INTEGER ,
  health_points INTEGER ,
  max_health_points INTEGER ,
  last_login_time TEXT ,
  total_play_time INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (player_id)
);

CREATE TABLE spell_casts (
  cast_id INTEGER ,
  player_id INTEGER ,
  spell_id INTEGER ,
  cast_time TEXT ,
  result TEXT ,
  mana_used INTEGER ,
  xp_gained INTEGER ,
  target_id INTEGER ,
  target_type TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (cast_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES players (player_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (spell_id) REFERENCES spells (spell_id)
);

CREATE TABLE classes (
  class_id INTEGER ,
  class_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  max_mana INTEGER ,
  starting_spells TEXT ,
  class_abilities TEXT ,
  icon TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (class_id)
);

CREATE TABLE spell_effects (
  effect_id INTEGER ,
  spell_id INTEGER ,
  effect_type TEXT ,
  effect_value INTEGER ,
  effect_duration INTEGER ,
  effect_description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (effect_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (spell_id) REFERENCES spells (spell_id)
);

CREATE TABLE spell_cooldowns (
  cooldown_id INTEGER ,
  spell_id INTEGER ,
  cooldown_duration INTEGER ,
  cooldown_start_time TEXT ,
  is_global_cooldown INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (cooldown_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (spell_id) REFERENCES spells (spell_id)
);

  PRIMARY KEY (note_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (spell_id) REFERENCES spells (spell_id)
);

-- Database: domain_name_dispute_resolution

CREATE TABLE cases (
  case_id INTEGER ,
  case_number TEXT ,
  domain TEXT ,
  complainant_id INTEGER ,
  respondent_id INTEGER ,
  ruleset INTEGER ,
  status INTEGER ,
  decision_date TEXT ,
  arbitrator_id INTEGER ,
  mediator_id INTEGER ,

  last_updated TEXT ,
  documents_url TEXT ,
  communication_log TEXT ,

  FOREIGN KEY (complainant_id) REFERENCES complainants (complainant_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (respondent_id) REFERENCES respondents (respondent_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (ruleset) REFERENCES rulesets (ruleset_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (status) REFERENCES statuses (status_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (arbitrator_id) REFERENCES arbitrators (arbitrator_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (mediator_id) REFERENCES mediators (mediator_id)
);

-- Database: airlines_operations_and_flight_management

CREATE TABLE flights (
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  flight_no TEXT ,
  origin TEXT ,
  destination TEXT ,
  departure_time TEXT ,
  ready_time TEXT ,
  capacity INTEGER ,
  aircraft_id INTEGER ,
  pilot_id INTEGER ,
  copilot_id INTEGER ,
  cabin_crew_ids TEXT ,
  gate_number TEXT ,
  baggage_claim_number TEXT ,
  estimated_arrival_time TEXT ,
  estimated_departure_time TEXT ,
  status TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (flight_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (origin) REFERENCES airports (iata_code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (destination) REFERENCES airports (iata_code), 
  FOREIGN KEY (aircraft_id) REFERENCES aircraft (aircraft_id)
);

CREATE TABLE airports (
  airport_id INTEGER ,
  iata_code TEXT ,
  name TEXT ,
  city TEXT ,
  country TEXT ,
  timezone TEXT ,
  latitude TEXT ,
  longitude TEXT ,
  elevation INTEGER ,
  timezone_offset TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (airport_id)
);

CREATE TABLE aircraft (
  aircraft_id INTEGER ,
  registration_number TEXT ,
  model TEXT ,
  capacity INTEGER ,
  maintenance_status TEXT ,
  manufacturer TEXT ,
  year_of_manufacture INTEGER ,
  last_maintenance_date TEXT ,
  next_maintenance_date TEXT ,
  total_flight_hours TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (aircraft_id)
);

CREATE TABLE flight_status (
  status_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  actual_departure_time TEXT ,
  actual_arrival_time TEXT ,
  departure_delay INTEGER ,
  arrival_delay INTEGER ,
  status TEXT ,
  gate_departure_time TEXT ,
  gate_arrival_time TEXT ,
  reason_for_delay TEXT ,
  cancellation_reason TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (status_id), 
  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

CREATE TABLE passenger_manifest (
  manifest_id INTEGER ,
  flight_id INTEGER ,
  passenger_id INTEGER ,
  booking_reference TEXT ,
  seat_number TEXT ,
  check_in_status TEXT ,
  passport_number TEXT ,
  nationality TEXT ,
  baggage_weight TEXT ,

  FOREIGN KEY (flight_id) REFERENCES flights (flight_id)
);

  FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users (user_id)

CREATE TABLE users (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  username TEXT ,
  password TEXT ,
  email TEXT ,
  full_name TEXT ,
  created_at TEXT ,
  updated_at TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE roles (
  role_id INTEGER ,
  role_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (
  user_id INTEGER ,
  role_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE traits (
  trait_id INTEGER ,
  trait_name TEXT ,
  description TEXT ,
  PRIMARY KEY (trait_id)
);

CREATE TABLE variety_traits (
  variety_id INTEGER ,
  trait_id INTEGER ,
  PRIMARY KEY (variety_id, trait_id)
);
